  57 LY_ERR 
  58 lyd_value_store(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lyd_value *val, const struct lysc_type *type, const void *value, 
  59         size_t value_len, ly_bool *dynamic, LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, void *prefix_data, uint32_t hints, 
  60         const struct lysc_node *ctx_node, ly_bool *incomplete) 
  61 { 
  62     LY_ERR ret; 
  63     struct ly_err_item *err = NULL; 
  64     uint32_t options = (dynamic && *dynamic ? LYPLG_TYPE_STORE_DYNAMIC : 0); 
  65  
  66     if (!value) { 
  67         value = ""; 
  68     } 
  69     if (incomplete) { 
  70         *incomplete = 0; 
  71     } 
  72  
  73     ret = type->plugin->store(ctx, type, value, value_len, options, format, prefix_data, hints, ctx_node, val, NULL, &err); 
  74     if (dynamic) { 
  75         *dynamic = 0; 
  76     } 
  77  
  78     if (ret == LY_EINCOMPLETE) { 
  79         if (incomplete) { 
  80             *incomplete = 1; 
  81         } 
  82     } else if (ret) { 
  83         if (err) { 
  84             LOGVAL_ERRITEM(ctx, err); 
  85             ly_err_free(err); 
  86         } else { 
  87             LOGVAL(ctx, LYVE_OTHER, "Storing value \"%.*s\" failed.", (int)value_len, value); 
  88         } 
  89         return ret; 
  90     } 
  91  
  92     return LY_SUCCESS; 
  93 } 
  94  
  95 LY_ERR 
  96 lyd_value_validate_incomplete(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const struct lysc_type *type, struct lyd_value *val, 
  97         const struct lyd_node *ctx_node, const struct lyd_node *tree) 
  98 { 
  99     LY_ERR ret; 
 100     struct ly_err_item *err = NULL; 
 101  
 102     assert(type->plugin->validate); 
 103  
 104     ret = type->plugin->validate(ctx, type, ctx_node, tree, val, &err); 
 105     if (ret) { 
 106         if (err) { 
 107             LOGVAL_ERRITEM(ctx, err); 
 108             ly_err_free(err); 
 109         } else { 
 110             LOGVAL(ctx, LYVE_OTHER, "Resolving value \"%s\" failed.", type->plugin->print(ctx, val, LY_VALUE_CANON, 
 111                     NULL, NULL, NULL)); 
 112         } 
 113         return ret; 
 114     } 
 115  
 116     return LY_SUCCESS; 
 117 }

 119 LY_ERR 
 120 lys_value_validate(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const struct lysc_node *node, const char *value, size_t value_len, 
 121         LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, void *prefix_data) 
 122 { 
 123     LY_ERR rc = LY_SUCCESS; 
 124     struct ly_err_item *err = NULL; 
 125     struct lyd_value storage; 
 126     struct lysc_type *type; 
 127  
 128     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, node, value, LY_EINVAL); 
 129  
 130     if (!(node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST))) { 
 131         LOGARG(ctx, node); 
 132         return LY_EINVAL; 
 133     } 
 134  
 135     type = ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)node)->type; 
 136     rc = type->plugin->store(ctx ? ctx : node->module->ctx, type, value, value_len, 0, format, prefix_data, 
 137             LYD_HINT_SCHEMA, node, &storage, NULL, &err); 
 138     if (rc == LY_EINCOMPLETE) { 
 139         /* actually success since we do not provide the context tree and call validation with 
 140          * LY_TYPE_OPTS_INCOMPLETE_DATA */ 
 141         rc = LY_SUCCESS; 
 142     } else if (rc && err) { 
 143         if (ctx) { 
 144             /* log only in case the ctx was provided as input parameter */ 
 145             LOG_LOCSET(NULL, NULL, err->path, NULL); 
 146             LOGVAL_ERRITEM(ctx, err); 
 147             LOG_LOCBACK(0, 0, 1, 0); 
 148         } 
 149         ly_err_free(err); 
 150     } 
 151  
 152     if (!rc) { 
 153         type->plugin->free(ctx ? ctx : node->module->ctx, &storage); 
 154     } 
 155     return rc; 
 156 }

 158 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 159 lyd_value_validate(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const struct lysc_node *schema, const char *value, size_t value_len, 
 160         const struct lyd_node *ctx_node, const struct lysc_type **realtype, const char **canonical) 
 161 { 
 162     LY_ERR rc; 
 163     struct ly_err_item *err = NULL; 
 164     struct lysc_type *type; 
 165     struct lyd_value val = {0}; 
 166     ly_bool stored = 0, log = 1; 
 167  
 168     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, schema, value, LY_EINVAL); 
 169  
 170     if (!ctx) { 
 171         ctx = schema->module->ctx; 
 172         log = 0; 
 173     } 
 174     type = ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)schema)->type; 
 175  
 176     /* store */ 
 177     rc = type->plugin->store(ctx, type, value, value_len, 0, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, 
 178             LYD_HINT_DATA, schema, &val, NULL, &err); 
 179     if (!rc || (rc == LY_EINCOMPLETE)) { 
 180         stored = 1; 
 181     } 
 182  
 183     if (ctx_node && (rc == LY_EINCOMPLETE)) { 
 184         /* resolve */ 
 185         rc = type->plugin->validate(ctx, type, ctx_node, ctx_node, &val, &err); 
 186     } 
 187  
 188     if (rc && (rc != LY_EINCOMPLETE) && err) { 
 189         if (log) { 
 190             /* log error */ 
 191             if (err->path) { 
 192                 LOG_LOCSET(NULL, NULL, err->path, NULL); 
 193             } else if (ctx_node) { 
 194                 LOG_LOCSET(NULL, ctx_node, NULL, NULL); 
 195             } else { 
 196                 LOG_LOCSET(schema, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
 197             } 
 198             LOGVAL_ERRITEM(ctx, err); 
 199             if (err->path) { 
 200                 LOG_LOCBACK(0, 0, 1, 0); 
 201             } else if (ctx_node) { 
 202                 LOG_LOCBACK(0, 1, 0, 0); 
 203             } else { 
 204                 LOG_LOCBACK(1, 0, 0, 0); 
 205             } 
 206         } 
 207         ly_err_free(err); 
 208     } 
 209  
 210     if (!rc || (rc == LY_EINCOMPLETE)) { 
 211         if (realtype) { 
 212             /* return realtype */ 
 213             if (val.realtype->basetype == LY_TYPE_UNION) { 
 214                 *realtype = val.subvalue->value.realtype; 
 215             } else { 
 216                 *realtype = val.realtype; 
 217             } 
 218         } 
 219  
 220         if (canonical) { 
 221             /* return canonical value */ 
 222             lydict_insert(ctx, val.realtype->plugin->print(ctx, &val, LY_VALUE_CANON, NULL, NULL, NULL), 0, canonical); 
 223         } 
 224     } 
 225  
 226     if (stored) { 
 227         /* free value */ 
 228         type->plugin->free(ctx ? ctx : schema->module->ctx, &val); 
 229     } 
 230     return rc; 
 231 }

 233 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 234 lyd_value_compare(const struct lyd_node_term *node, const char *value, size_t value_len) 
 235 { 
 236     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
 237     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
 238     struct lysc_type *type; 
 239     struct lyd_value val = {0}; 
 240  
 241     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(node ? node->schema->module->ctx : NULL, node, value, LY_EINVAL); 
 242  
 243     ctx = node->schema->module->ctx; 
 244     type = ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)node->schema)->type; 
 245  
 246     /* store the value */ 
 247     LOG_LOCSET(node->schema, &node->node, NULL, NULL); 
 248     ret = lyd_value_store(ctx, &val, type, value, value_len, NULL, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, node->schema, NULL); 
 249     LOG_LOCBACK(1, 1, 0, 0); 
 250     LY_CHECK_RET(ret); 
 251  
 252     /* compare values */ 
 253     ret = type->plugin->compare(&node->value, &val); 
 254  
 255     type->plugin->free(ctx, &val); 
 256     return ret; 
 257 }

 259 LIBYANG_API_DEF ly_bool 
 260 lyd_is_default(const struct lyd_node *node) 
 261 { 
 262     const struct lysc_node_leaf *leaf; 
 263     const struct lysc_node_leaflist *llist; 
 264     const struct lyd_node_term *term; 
 265     LY_ARRAY_COUNT_TYPE u; 
 266  
 267     if (!(node->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM)) { 
 268         return 0; 
 269     } 
 270  
 271     term = (const struct lyd_node_term *)node; 
 272  
 273     if (node->schema->nodetype == LYS_LEAF) { 
 274         leaf = (const struct lysc_node_leaf *)node->schema; 
 275         if (!leaf->dflt) { 
 276             return 0; 
 277         } 
 278  
 279         /* compare with the default value */ 
 280         if (!leaf->type->plugin->compare(&term->value, leaf->dflt)) { 
 281             return 1; 
 282         } 
 283     } else { 
 284         llist = (const struct lysc_node_leaflist *)node->schema; 
 285         if (!llist->dflts) { 
 286             return 0; 
 287         } 
 288  
 289         LY_ARRAY_FOR(llist->dflts, u) { 
 290             /* compare with each possible default value */ 
 291             if (!llist->type->plugin->compare(&term->value, llist->dflts[u])) { 
 292                 return 1; 
 293             } 
 294         } 
 295     } 
 296  
 297     return 0; 
 298 }

 300 static LYD_FORMAT 
 301 lyd_parse_get_format(const struct ly_in *in, LYD_FORMAT format) 
 302 { 
 303     if (!format && (in->type == LY_IN_FILEPATH)) { 
 304         /* unknown format - try to detect it from filename's suffix */ 
 305         const char *path = in->method.fpath.filepath; 
 306         size_t len = strlen(path); 
 307  
 308         /* ignore trailing whitespaces */ 
 309         for ( ; len > 0 && isspace(path[len - 1]); len--) {} 
 310  
 311         if ((len >= LY_XML_SUFFIX_LEN + 1) && 
 312                 !strncmp(&path[len - LY_XML_SUFFIX_LEN], LY_XML_SUFFIX, LY_XML_SUFFIX_LEN)) { 
 313             format = LYD_XML; 
 314         } else if ((len >= LY_JSON_SUFFIX_LEN + 1) && 
 315                 !strncmp(&path[len - LY_JSON_SUFFIX_LEN], LY_JSON_SUFFIX, LY_JSON_SUFFIX_LEN)) { 
 316             format = LYD_JSON; 
 317         } else if ((len >= LY_LYB_SUFFIX_LEN + 1) && 
 318                 !strncmp(&path[len - LY_LYB_SUFFIX_LEN], LY_LYB_SUFFIX, LY_LYB_SUFFIX_LEN)) { 
 319             format = LYD_LYB; 
 320         } /* else still unknown */ 
 321     } 
 322  
 323     return format; 
 324 }

 326 /** 
 327  * @brief Parse YANG data into a data tree. 
 328  * 
 329  * @param[in] ctx libyang context. 
 330  * @param[in] ext Optional extenion instance to parse data following the schema tree specified in the extension instance 
 331  * @param[in] parent Parent to connect the parsed nodes to, if any. 
 332  * @param[in,out] first_p Pointer to the first top-level parsed node, used only if @p parent is NULL. 
 333  * @param[in] in Input handle to read the input from. 
 334  * @param[in] format Expected format of the data in @p in. 
 335  * @param[in] parse_opts Options for parser. 
 336  * @param[in] val_opts Options for validation. 
 337  * @param[out] op Optional pointer to the parsed operation, if any. 
 338  * @return LY_ERR value. 
 339  */ 
 340 static LY_ERR 
 341 lyd_parse(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_node **first_p, 
 342         struct ly_in *in, LYD_FORMAT format, uint32_t parse_opts, uint32_t val_opts, struct lyd_node **op) 
 343 { 
 344     LY_ERR rc = LY_SUCCESS; 
 345     struct lyd_ctx *lydctx = NULL; 
 346     struct ly_set parsed = {0}; 
 347     struct lyd_node *first; 
 348     uint32_t i; 
 349     ly_bool subtree_sibling = 0; 
 350  
 351     assert(ctx && (parent || first_p)); 
 352  
 353     format = lyd_parse_get_format(in, format); 
 354     if (first_p) { 
 355         *first_p = NULL; 
 356     } 
 357  
 358     /* remember input position */ 
 359     in->func_start = in->current; 
 360  
 361     /* parse the data */ 
 362     switch (format) { 
 363     case LYD_XML: 
 364         rc = lyd_parse_xml(ctx, ext, parent, first_p, in, parse_opts, val_opts, LYD_TYPE_DATA_YANG, NULL, &parsed, 
 365                 &subtree_sibling, &lydctx); 
 366         break; 
 367     case LYD_JSON: 
 368         rc = lyd_parse_json(ctx, ext, parent, first_p, in, parse_opts, val_opts, LYD_TYPE_DATA_YANG, &parsed, 
 369                 &subtree_sibling, &lydctx); 
 370         break; 
 371     case LYD_LYB: 
 372         rc = lyd_parse_lyb(ctx, ext, parent, first_p, in, parse_opts, val_opts, LYD_TYPE_DATA_YANG, &parsed, 
 373                 &subtree_sibling, &lydctx); 
 374         break; 
 375     case LYD_UNKNOWN: 
 376         LOGARG(ctx, format); 
 377         rc = LY_EINVAL; 
 378         break; 
 379     } 
 380     LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc, cleanup); 
 381  
 382     if (parent) { 
 383         /* get first top-level sibling */ 
 384         for (first = parent; first->parent; first = lyd_parent(first)) {} 
 385         first = lyd_first_sibling(first); 
 386         first_p = &first; 
 387     } 
 388  
 389     if (!(parse_opts & LYD_PARSE_ONLY)) { 
 390         /* validate data */ 
 391         rc = lyd_validate(first_p, NULL, ctx, val_opts, 0, &lydctx->node_when, &lydctx->node_types, &lydctx->meta_types, 
 392                 &lydctx->ext_val, NULL); 
 393         LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc, cleanup); 
 394     } 
 395  
 396     /* set the operation node */ 
 397     if (op) { 
 398         *op = lydctx->op_node; 
 399     } 
 400  
 401 cleanup: 
 402     if (lydctx) { 
 403         lydctx->free(lydctx); 
 404     } 
 405     if (rc) { 
 406         if (parent) { 
 407             /* free all the parsed subtrees */ 
 408             for (i = 0; i < parsed.count; ++i) { 
 409                 lyd_free_tree(parsed.dnodes[i]); 
 410             } 
 411         } else { 
 412             /* free everything */ 
 413             lyd_free_all(*first_p); 
 414             *first_p = NULL; 
 415         } 
 416     } else if (subtree_sibling) { 
 417         rc = LY_ENOT; 
 418     } 
 419     ly_set_erase(&parsed, NULL); 
 420     return rc; 
 421 }

 423 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 424 lyd_parse_ext_data(const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, struct lyd_node *parent, struct ly_in *in, LYD_FORMAT format, 
 425         uint32_t parse_options, uint32_t validate_options, struct lyd_node **tree) 
 426 { 
 427     const struct ly_ctx *ctx = ext ? ext->module->ctx : NULL; 
 428  
 429     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ext, in, parent || tree, LY_EINVAL); 
 430     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, !(parse_options & ~LYD_PARSE_OPTS_MASK), LY_EINVAL); 
 431     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, !(validate_options & ~LYD_VALIDATE_OPTS_MASK), LY_EINVAL); 
 432  
 433     return lyd_parse(ctx, ext, parent, tree, in, format, parse_options, validate_options, NULL); 
 434 }

 436 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 437 lyd_parse_data(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lyd_node *parent, struct ly_in *in, LYD_FORMAT format, 
 438         uint32_t parse_options, uint32_t validate_options, struct lyd_node **tree) 
 439 { 
 440     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ctx, in, parent || tree, LY_EINVAL); 
 441     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, !(parse_options & ~LYD_PARSE_OPTS_MASK), LY_EINVAL); 
 442     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, !(validate_options & ~LYD_VALIDATE_OPTS_MASK), LY_EINVAL); 
 443  
 444     return lyd_parse(ctx, NULL, parent, tree, in, format, parse_options, validate_options, NULL); 
 445 }

 447 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 448 lyd_parse_data_mem(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *data, LYD_FORMAT format, uint32_t parse_options, 
 449         uint32_t validate_options, struct lyd_node **tree) 
 450 { 
 451     LY_ERR ret; 
 452     struct ly_in *in; 
 453  
 454     LY_CHECK_RET(ly_in_new_memory(data, &in)); 
 455     ret = lyd_parse_data(ctx, NULL, in, format, parse_options, validate_options, tree); 
 456  
 457     ly_in_free(in, 0); 
 458     return ret; 
 459 }

 489 /** 
 490  * @brief Parse YANG data into an operation data tree, in case the extension instance is specified, keep the searching 
 491  * for schema nodes locked inside the extension instance. 
 492  * 
 493  * At least one of @p parent, @p tree, or @p op must always be set. 
 494  * 
 495  * Specific @p data_type values have different parameter meaning as follows: 
 496  * - ::LYD_TYPE_RPC_NETCONF: 
 497  *   - @p parent - must be NULL, the whole RPC is expected; 
 498  *   - @p format - must be ::LYD_XML, NETCONF supports only this format; 
 499  *   - @p tree - must be provided, all the NETCONF-specific XML envelopes will be returned here as 
 500  *               a separate opaque data tree, even if the function fails, this may be returned; 
 501  *   - @p op - must be provided, the RPC/action data tree itself will be returned here, pointing to the operation; 
 502  * 
 503  * - ::LYD_TYPE_NOTIF_NETCONF: 
 504  *   - @p parent - must be NULL, the whole notification is expected; 
 505  *   - @p format - must be ::LYD_XML, NETCONF supports only this format; 
 506  *   - @p tree - must be provided, all the NETCONF-specific XML envelopes will be returned here as 
 507  *               a separate opaque data tree, even if the function fails, this may be returned; 
 508  *   - @p op - must be provided, the notification data tree itself will be returned here, pointing to the operation; 
 509  * 
 510  * - ::LYD_TYPE_REPLY_NETCONF: 
 511  *   - @p parent - must be set, pointing to the invoked RPC operation (RPC or action) node; 
 512  *   - @p format - must be ::LYD_XML, NETCONF supports only this format; 
 513  *   - @p tree - must be provided, all the NETCONF-specific XML envelopes will be returned here as 
 514  *               a separate opaque data tree, even if the function fails, this may be returned; 
 515  *   - @p op - must be NULL, the reply is appended to the RPC; 
 516  *   Note that there are 3 kinds of NETCONF replies - ok, error, and data. Only data reply appends any nodes to the RPC. 
 517  * 
 518  * @param[in] ctx libyang context. 
 519  * @param[in] ext Extension instance providing the specific schema tree to match with the data being parsed. 
 520  * @param[in] parent Optional parent to connect the parsed nodes to. 
 521  * @param[in] in Input handle to read the input from. 
 522  * @param[in] format Expected format of the data in @p in. 
 523  * @param[in] data_type Expected operation to parse (@ref datatype). 
 524  * @param[out] tree Optional full parsed data tree. If @p parent is set, set to NULL. 
 525  * @param[out] op Optional parsed operation node. 
 526  * @return LY_ERR value. 
 527  * @return LY_ENOT if @p data_type is a NETCONF message and the root XML element is not the expected one. 
 528  */ 
 529 static LY_ERR 
 530 lyd_parse_op_(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, struct lyd_node *parent, 
 531         struct ly_in *in, LYD_FORMAT format, enum lyd_type data_type, struct lyd_node **tree, struct lyd_node **op) 
 532 { 
 533     LY_ERR rc = LY_SUCCESS; 
 534     struct lyd_ctx *lydctx = NULL; 
 535     struct ly_set parsed = {0}; 
 536     struct lyd_node *first = NULL, *envp = NULL; 
 537     uint32_t i, parse_opts, val_opts; 
 538  
 539     if (!ctx) { 
 540         ctx = LYD_CTX(parent); 
 541     } 
 542     if (tree) { 
 543         *tree = NULL; 
 544     } 
 545     if (op) { 
 546         *op = NULL; 
 547     } 
 548  
 549     format = lyd_parse_get_format(in, format); 
 550  
 551     /* remember input position */ 
 552     in->func_start = in->current; 
 553  
 554     /* check params based on the data type */ 
 555     if ((data_type == LYD_TYPE_RPC_NETCONF) || (data_type == LYD_TYPE_NOTIF_NETCONF)) { 
 556         LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, format == LYD_XML, !parent, tree, op, LY_EINVAL); 
 557     } else if (data_type == LYD_TYPE_REPLY_NETCONF) { 
 558         LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, format == LYD_XML, parent, parent->schema->nodetype & (LYS_RPC | LYS_ACTION), tree, !op, 
 559                 LY_EINVAL); 
 560     } 
 561     parse_opts = LYD_PARSE_ONLY | LYD_PARSE_OPAQ; 
 562     val_opts = 0; 
 563  
 564     /* parse the data */ 
 565     switch (format) { 
 566     case LYD_XML: 
 567         rc = lyd_parse_xml(ctx, ext, parent, &first, in, parse_opts, val_opts, data_type, &envp, &parsed, NULL, &lydctx); 
 568         if (rc && envp) { 
 569             /* special situation when the envelopes were parsed successfully */ 
 570             if (tree) { 
 571                 *tree = envp; 
 572             } 
 573             ly_set_erase(&parsed, NULL); 
 574             return rc; 
 575         } 
 576         break; 
 577     case LYD_JSON: 
 578         rc = lyd_parse_json(ctx, ext, parent, &first, in, parse_opts, val_opts, data_type, &parsed, NULL, &lydctx); 
 579         break; 
 580     case LYD_LYB: 
 581         rc = lyd_parse_lyb(ctx, ext, parent, &first, in, parse_opts, val_opts, data_type, &parsed, NULL, &lydctx); 
 582         break; 
 583     case LYD_UNKNOWN: 
 584         LOGARG(ctx, format); 
 585         rc = LY_EINVAL; 
 586         break; 
 587     } 
 588     LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc, cleanup); 
 589  
 590     /* set out params correctly */ 
 591     if (tree) { 
 592         if (envp) { 
 593             /* special out param meaning */ 
 594             *tree = envp; 
 595         } else { 
 596             *tree = parent ? NULL : first; 
 597         } 
 598     } 
 599     if (op) { 
 600         *op = lydctx->op_node; 
 601     } 
 602  
 603 cleanup: 
 604     if (lydctx) { 
 605         lydctx->free(lydctx); 
 606     } 
 607     if (rc) { 
 608         if (parent) { 
 609             /* free all the parsed subtrees */ 
 610             for (i = 0; i < parsed.count; ++i) { 
 611                 lyd_free_tree(parsed.dnodes[i]); 
 612             } 
 613         } else { 
 614             /* free everything (cannot occur in the current code, a safety) */ 
 615             lyd_free_all(first); 
 616             if (tree) { 
 617                 *tree = NULL; 
 618             } 
 619             if (op) { 
 620                 *op = NULL; 
 621             } 
 622         } 
 623     } 
 624     ly_set_erase(&parsed, NULL); 
 625     return rc; 
 626 } 
 627  
 628 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 629 lyd_parse_op(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lyd_node *parent, struct ly_in *in, LYD_FORMAT format, 
 630         enum lyd_type data_type, struct lyd_node **tree, struct lyd_node **op) 
 631 { 
 632     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ctx || parent, in, data_type, parent || tree || op, LY_EINVAL); 
 633  
 634     return lyd_parse_op_(ctx, NULL, parent, in, format, data_type, tree, op); 
 635 } 
 636  
 637 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 638 lyd_parse_ext_op(const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, struct lyd_node *parent, struct ly_in *in, LYD_FORMAT format, 
 639         enum lyd_type data_type, struct lyd_node **tree, struct lyd_node **op) 
 640 { 
 641     const struct ly_ctx *ctx = ext ? ext->module->ctx : NULL; 
 642  
 643     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ext, in, data_type, parent || tree || op, LY_EINVAL); 
 644  
 645     return lyd_parse_op_(ctx, ext, parent, in, format, data_type, tree, op); 
 646 } 
 647  
 648 LY_ERR 
 649 lyd_create_term(const struct lysc_node *schema, const char *value, size_t value_len, ly_bool *dynamic, 
 650         LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, void *prefix_data, uint32_t hints, ly_bool *incomplete, struct lyd_node **node) 
 651 { 
 652     LY_ERR ret; 
 653     struct lyd_node_term *term; 
 654  
 655     assert(schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM); 
 656  
 657     term = calloc(1, sizeof *term); 
 658     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!term, LOGMEM(schema->module->ctx), LY_EMEM); 
 659  
 660     term->schema = schema; 
 661     term->prev = &term->node; 
 662     term->flags = LYD_NEW; 
 663  
 664     LOG_LOCSET(schema, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
 665     ret = lyd_value_store(schema->module->ctx, &term->value, ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)term->schema)->type, value, 
 666             value_len, dynamic, format, prefix_data, hints, schema, incomplete); 
 667     LOG_LOCBACK(1, 0, 0, 0); 
 668     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(ret, free(term), ret); 
 669     lyd_hash(&term->node); 
 670  
 671     *node = &term->node; 
 672     return ret; 
 673 } 
 674  
 675 LY_ERR 
 676 lyd_create_term2(const struct lysc_node *schema, const struct lyd_value *val, struct lyd_node **node) 
 677 { 
 678     LY_ERR ret; 
 679     struct lyd_node_term *term; 
 680     struct lysc_type *type; 
 681  
 682     assert(schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM); 
 683     assert(val && val->realtype); 
 684  
 685     term = calloc(1, sizeof *term); 
 686     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!term, LOGMEM(schema->module->ctx), LY_EMEM); 
 687  
 688     term->schema = schema; 
 689     term->prev = &term->node; 
 690     term->flags = LYD_NEW; 
 691  
 692     type = ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)schema)->type; 
 693     ret = type->plugin->duplicate(schema->module->ctx, val, &term->value); 
 694     if (ret) { 
 695         LOGERR(schema->module->ctx, ret, "Value duplication failed."); 
 696         free(term); 
 697         return ret; 
 698     } 
 699     lyd_hash(&term->node); 
 700  
 701     *node = &term->node; 
 702     return ret; 
 703 } 
 704  
 705 LY_ERR 
 706 lyd_create_inner(const struct lysc_node *schema, struct lyd_node **node) 
 707 { 
 708     struct lyd_node_inner *in; 
 709  
 710     assert(schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_INNER); 
 711  
 712     in = calloc(1, sizeof *in); 
 713     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!in, LOGMEM(schema->module->ctx), LY_EMEM); 
 714  
 715     in->schema = schema; 
 716     in->prev = &in->node; 
 717     in->flags = LYD_NEW; 
 718     if ((schema->nodetype == LYS_CONTAINER) && !(schema->flags & LYS_PRESENCE)) { 
 719         in->flags |= LYD_DEFAULT; 
 720     } 
 721  
 722     /* do not hash list with keys, we need them for the hash */ 
 723     if ((schema->nodetype != LYS_LIST) || (schema->flags & LYS_KEYLESS)) { 
 724         lyd_hash(&in->node); 
 725     } 
 726  
 727     *node = &in->node; 
 728     return LY_SUCCESS; 
 729 } 
 730  
 731 LY_ERR 
 732 lyd_create_list(const struct lysc_node *schema, const struct ly_path_predicate *predicates, struct lyd_node **node) 
 733 { 
 734     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
 735     struct lyd_node *list = NULL, *key; 
 736     LY_ARRAY_COUNT_TYPE u; 
 737  
 738     assert((schema->nodetype == LYS_LIST) && !(schema->flags & LYS_KEYLESS)); 
 739  
 740     /* create list */ 
 741     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_inner(schema, &list), cleanup); 
 742  
 743     LOG_LOCSET(NULL, list, NULL, NULL); 
 744  
 745     /* create and insert all the keys */ 
 746     LY_ARRAY_FOR(predicates, u) { 
 747         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_term2(predicates[u].key, &predicates[u].value, &key), cleanup); 
 748         lyd_insert_node(list, NULL, key, 0); 
 749     } 
 750  
 751     /* hash having all the keys */ 
 752     lyd_hash(list); 
 753  
 754     /* success */ 
 755     *node = list; 
 756     list = NULL; 
 757  
 758 cleanup: 
 759     LOG_LOCBACK(0, 1, 0, 0); 
 760     lyd_free_tree(list); 
 761     return ret; 
 762 } 
 763  
 764 static LY_ERR 
 765 lyd_create_list2(const struct lysc_node *schema, const char *keys, size_t keys_len, struct lyd_node **node) 
 766 { 
 767     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
 768     struct lyxp_expr *expr = NULL; 
 769     uint16_t exp_idx = 0; 
 770     enum ly_path_pred_type pred_type = 0; 
 771     struct ly_path_predicate *predicates = NULL; 
 772  
 773     LOG_LOCSET(schema, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
 774  
 775     /* parse keys */ 
 776     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = ly_path_parse_predicate(schema->module->ctx, NULL, keys, keys_len, LY_PATH_PREFIX_OPTIONAL, 
 777             LY_PATH_PRED_KEYS, &expr), cleanup); 
 778  
 779     /* compile them */ 
 780     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = ly_path_compile_predicate(schema->module->ctx, NULL, NULL, schema, expr, &exp_idx, LY_VALUE_JSON, 
 781             NULL, &predicates, &pred_type), cleanup); 
 782  
 783     /* create the list node */ 
 784     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_list(schema, predicates, node), cleanup); 
 785  
 786 cleanup: 
 787     LOG_LOCBACK(1, 0, 0, 0); 
 788     lyxp_expr_free(schema->module->ctx, expr); 
 789     ly_path_predicates_free(schema->module->ctx, pred_type, predicates); 
 790     return ret; 
 791 } 
 792  
 793 LY_ERR 
 794 lyd_create_any(const struct lysc_node *schema, const void *value, LYD_ANYDATA_VALUETYPE value_type, ly_bool use_value, 
 795         struct lyd_node **node) 
 796 { 
 797     LY_ERR ret; 
 798     struct lyd_node_any *any; 
 799     union lyd_any_value any_val; 
 800  
 801     assert(schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_ANY); 
 802  
 803     any = calloc(1, sizeof *any); 
 804     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!any, LOGMEM(schema->module->ctx), LY_EMEM); 
 805  
 806     any->schema = schema; 
 807     any->prev = &any->node; 
 808     any->flags = LYD_NEW; 
 809  
 810     /* TODO: convert XML/JSON strings into a opaq data tree */ 
 811  
 812     if (use_value) { 
 813         any->value.str = value; 
 814         any->value_type = value_type; 
 815     } else { 
 816         any_val.str = value; 
 817         ret = lyd_any_copy_value(&any->node, &any_val, value_type); 
 818         LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(ret, free(any), ret); 
 819     } 
 820     lyd_hash(&any->node); 
 821  
 822     *node = &any->node; 
 823     return LY_SUCCESS; 
 824 } 
 825  
 826 LY_ERR 
 827 lyd_create_opaq(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *name, size_t name_len, const char *prefix, size_t pref_len, 
 828         const char *module_key, size_t module_key_len, const char *value, size_t value_len, ly_bool *dynamic, 
 829         LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, void *val_prefix_data, uint32_t hints, struct lyd_node **node) 
 830 { 
 831     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
 832     struct lyd_node_opaq *opaq; 
 833  
 834     assert(ctx && name && name_len && format); 
 835  
 836     if (!value_len && (!dynamic || !*dynamic)) { 
 837         value = ""; 
 838     } 
 839  
 840     opaq = calloc(1, sizeof *opaq); 
 841     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!opaq, LOGMEM(ctx); ret = LY_EMEM, finish); 
 842  
 843     opaq->prev = &opaq->node; 
 844     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, name, name_len, &opaq->name.name), finish); 
 845  
 846     if (pref_len) { 
 847         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, prefix, pref_len, &opaq->name.prefix), finish); 
 848     } 
 849     if (module_key_len) { 
 850         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, module_key, module_key_len, &opaq->name.module_ns), finish); 
 851     } 
 852     if (dynamic && *dynamic) { 
 853         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert_zc(ctx, (char *)value, &opaq->value), finish); 
 854         *dynamic = 0; 
 855     } else { 
 856         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, value, value_len, &opaq->value), finish); 
 857     } 
 858  
 859     opaq->format = format; 
 860     opaq->val_prefix_data = val_prefix_data; 
 861     opaq->hints = hints; 
 862     opaq->ctx = ctx; 
 863  
 864 finish: 
 865     if (ret) { 
 866         lyd_free_tree(&opaq->node); 
 867         ly_free_prefix_data(format, val_prefix_data); 
 868     } else { 
 869         *node = &opaq->node; 
 870     } 
 871     return ret; 
 872 } 
 873  
 874 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 875 lyd_new_inner(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, ly_bool output, 
 876         struct lyd_node **node) 
 877 { 
 878     struct lyd_node *ret = NULL; 
 879     const struct lysc_node *schema; 
 880     const struct ly_ctx *ctx = parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : (module ? module->ctx : NULL); 
 881  
 882     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, parent || module, parent || node, name, LY_EINVAL); 
 883     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, module ? module->ctx : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
 884  
 885     if (!module) { 
 886         module = parent->schema->module; 
 887     } 
 888  
 889     schema = lys_find_child(parent ? parent->schema : NULL, module, name, 0, 
 890             LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_NOTIF | LYS_RPC | LYS_ACTION, output ? LYS_GETNEXT_OUTPUT : 0); 
 891     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!schema, LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Inner node (not a list) \"%s\" not found.", name), LY_ENOTFOUND); 
 892  
 893     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_inner(schema, &ret)); 
 894     if (parent) { 
 895         lyd_insert_node(parent, NULL, ret, 0); 
 896     } 
 897  
 898     if (node) { 
 899         *node = ret; 
 900     } 
 901     return LY_SUCCESS; 
 902 } 
 903  
 904 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 905 lyd_new_ext_inner(const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, const char *name, struct lyd_node **node) 
 906 { 
 907     struct lyd_node *ret = NULL; 
 908     const struct lysc_node *schema; 
 909     struct ly_ctx *ctx = ext ? ext->module->ctx : NULL; 
 910  
 911     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ext, node, name, LY_EINVAL); 
 912  
 913     schema = lysc_ext_find_node(ext, NULL, name, 0, LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_NOTIF | LYS_RPC | LYS_ACTION, 0); 
 914     if (!schema) { 
 915         if (ext->argument) { 
 916             LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Inner node (not a list) \"%s\" not found in instance \"%s\" of extension %s.", 
 917                     name, ext->argument, ext->def->name); 
 918         } else { 
 919             LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Inner node (not a list)  \"%s\" not found in instance of extension %s.", 
 920                     name, ext->def->name); 
 921         } 
 922         return LY_ENOTFOUND; 
 923     } 
 924     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_inner(schema, &ret)); 
 925  
 926     *node = ret; 
 927  
 928     return LY_SUCCESS; 
 929 } 
 930  
 931 /** 
 932  * @brief Create a new list node in the data tree. 
 933  * 
 934  * @param[in] parent Parent node for the node being created. NULL in case of creating a top level element. 
 935  * @param[in] module Module of the node being created. If NULL, @p parent module will be used. 
 936  * @param[in] name Schema node name of the new data node. The node must be #LYS_LIST. 
 937  * @param[in] format Format of key values. 
 938  * @param[in] output Flag in case the @p parent is RPC/Action. If value is 0, the input's data nodes of the RPC/Action are 
 939  * taken into consideration. Otherwise, the output's data node is going to be created. 
 940  * @param[out] node Optional created node. 
 941  * @param[in] ap Ordered key values of the new list instance, all must be set. For ::LY_VALUE_LYB, every value must 
 942  * be followed by the value length. 
 943  * @return LY_ERR value. 
 944  */ 
 945 static LY_ERR 
 946 _lyd_new_list(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, 
 947         ly_bool output, struct lyd_node **node, va_list ap) 
 948 { 
 949     struct lyd_node *ret = NULL, *key; 
 950     const struct lysc_node *schema, *key_s; 
 951     const struct ly_ctx *ctx = parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : (module ? module->ctx : NULL); 
 952     const void *key_val; 
 953     uint32_t key_len; 
 954     LY_ERR rc = LY_SUCCESS; 
 955  
 956     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, parent || module, parent || node, name, LY_EINVAL); 
 957     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, module ? module->ctx : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
 958  
 959     if (!module) { 
 960         module = parent->schema->module; 
 961     } 
 962  
 963     schema = lys_find_child(parent ? parent->schema : NULL, module, name, 0, LYS_LIST, output ? LYS_GETNEXT_OUTPUT : 0); 
 964     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!schema, LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "List node \"%s\" not found.", name), LY_ENOTFOUND); 
 965  
 966     /* create list inner node */ 
 967     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_inner(schema, &ret)); 
 968  
 969     /* create and insert all the keys */ 
 970     for (key_s = lysc_node_child(schema); key_s && (key_s->flags & LYS_KEY); key_s = key_s->next) { 
 971         if (format == LY_VALUE_LYB) { 
 972             key_val = va_arg(ap, const void *); 
 973             key_len = va_arg(ap, uint32_t); 
 974         } else { 
 975             key_val = va_arg(ap, const char *); 
 976             key_len = key_val ? strlen((char *)key_val) : 0; 
 977         } 
 978  
 979         rc = lyd_create_term(key_s, key_val, key_len, NULL, format, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, NULL, &key); 
 980         LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc, cleanup); 
 981         lyd_insert_node(ret, NULL, key, 1); 
 982     } 
 983  
 984     if (parent) { 
 985         lyd_insert_node(parent, NULL, ret, 0); 
 986     } 
 987  
 988 cleanup: 
 989     if (rc) { 
 990         lyd_free_tree(ret); 
 991         ret = NULL; 
 992     } else if (node) { 
 993         *node = ret; 
 994     } 
 995     return rc; 
 996 } 
 997  
 998 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
 999 lyd_new_list(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, ly_bool output, 
1000         struct lyd_node **node, ...) 
1001 { 
1002     LY_ERR rc; 
1003     va_list ap; 
1004  
1005     va_start(ap, node); 
1006  
1007     rc = _lyd_new_list(parent, module, name, LY_VALUE_JSON, output, node, ap); 
1008  
1009     va_end(ap); 
1010     return rc; 
1011 }

1019

1023

1043 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1044 lyd_new_ext_list(const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, const char *name, struct lyd_node **node, ...) 
1045 { 
1046     struct lyd_node *ret = NULL, *key; 
1047     const struct lysc_node *schema, *key_s; 
1048     struct ly_ctx *ctx = ext ? ext->module->ctx : NULL; 
1049     va_list ap; 
1050     const char *key_val; 
1051     LY_ERR rc = LY_SUCCESS; 
1052  
1053     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ext, node, name, LY_EINVAL); 
1054  
1055     schema = lysc_ext_find_node(ext, NULL, name, 0, LYS_LIST, 0); 
1056     if (!schema) { 
1057         if (ext->argument) { 
1058             LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "List node \"%s\" not found in instance \"%s\" of extension %s.", 
1059                     name, ext->argument, ext->def->name); 
1060         } else { 
1061             LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "List node \"%s\" not found in instance of extension %s.", name, ext->def->name); 
1062         } 
1063         return LY_ENOTFOUND; 
1064     } 
1065     /* create list inner node */ 
1066     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_inner(schema, &ret)); 
1067  
1068     va_start(ap, node); 
1069  
1070     /* create and insert all the keys */ 
1071     for (key_s = lysc_node_child(schema); key_s && (key_s->flags & LYS_KEY); key_s = key_s->next) { 
1072         key_val = va_arg(ap, const char *); 
1073  
1074         rc = lyd_create_term(key_s, key_val, key_val ? strlen(key_val) : 0, NULL, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, 
1075                 NULL, &key); 
1076         LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc, cleanup); 
1077         lyd_insert_node(ret, NULL, key, 1); 
1078     } 
1079  
1080 cleanup: 
1081     va_end(ap); 
1082     if (rc) { 
1083         lyd_free_tree(ret); 
1084         ret = NULL; 
1085     } 
1086     *node = ret; 
1087     return rc; 
1088 } 
1089  
1090 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1091 lyd_new_list2(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, const char *keys, 
1092         ly_bool output, struct lyd_node **node) 
1093 { 
1094     struct lyd_node *ret = NULL; 
1095     const struct lysc_node *schema; 
1096     const struct ly_ctx *ctx = parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : (module ? module->ctx : NULL); 
1097  
1098     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, parent || module, parent || node, name, LY_EINVAL); 
1099     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, module ? module->ctx : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
1100  
1101     if (!module) { 
1102         module = parent->schema->module; 
1103     } 
1104     if (!keys) { 
1105         keys = ""; 
1106     } 
1107  
1108     /* find schema node */ 
1109     schema = lys_find_child(parent ? parent->schema : NULL, module, name, 0, LYS_LIST, output ? LYS_GETNEXT_OUTPUT : 0); 
1110     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!schema, LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "List node \"%s\" not found.", name), LY_ENOTFOUND); 
1111  
1112     if ((schema->flags & LYS_KEYLESS) && !keys[0]) { 
1113         /* key-less list */ 
1114         LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_inner(schema, &ret)); 
1115     } else { 
1116         /* create the list node */ 
1117         LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_list2(schema, keys, strlen(keys), &ret)); 
1118     } 
1119     if (parent) { 
1120         lyd_insert_node(parent, NULL, ret, 0); 
1121     } 
1122  
1123     if (node) { 
1124         *node = ret; 
1125     } 
1126     return LY_SUCCESS; 
1127 } 
1128  
1129 /** 
1130  * @brief Create a new term node in the data tree. 
1131  * 
1132  * @param[in] parent Parent node for the node being created. NULL in case of creating a top level element. 
1133  * @param[in] module Module of the node being created. If NULL, @p parent module will be used. 
1134  * @param[in] name Schema node name of the new data node. The node can be ::LYS_LEAF or ::LYS_LEAFLIST. 
1135  * @param[in] value Value of the node being created. 
1136  * @param[in] value_len Length of @p value. 
1137  * @param[in] format Format of @p value. 
1138  * @param[in] output Flag in case the @p parent is RPC/Action. If value is 0, the input's data nodes of the RPC/Action are 
1139  * taken into consideration. Otherwise, the output's data node is going to be created. 
1140  * @param[out] node Optional created node. 
1141  * @return LY_ERR value. 
1142  */ 
1143 static LY_ERR 
1144 _lyd_new_term(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, const void *value, 
1145         size_t value_len, LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, ly_bool output, struct lyd_node **node) 
1146 { 
1147     LY_ERR rc; 
1148     struct lyd_node *ret = NULL; 
1149     const struct lysc_node *schema; 
1150     const struct ly_ctx *ctx = parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : (module ? module->ctx : NULL); 
1151  
1152     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, parent || module, parent || node, name, LY_EINVAL); 
1153     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, module ? module->ctx : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
1154  
1155     if (!module) { 
1156         module = parent->schema->module; 
1157     } 
1158  
1159     schema = lys_find_child(parent ? parent->schema : NULL, module, name, 0, LYD_NODE_TERM, output ? LYS_GETNEXT_OUTPUT : 0); 
1160     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!schema, LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Term node \"%s\" not found.", name), LY_ENOTFOUND); 
1161  
1162     rc = lyd_create_term(schema, value, value_len, NULL, format, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, NULL, &ret); 
1163     LY_CHECK_RET(rc); 
1164     if (parent) { 
1165         lyd_insert_node(parent, NULL, ret, 0); 
1166     } 
1167  
1168     if (node) { 
1169         *node = ret; 
1170     } 
1171     return LY_SUCCESS; 
1172 } 
1173

1175 lyd_new_term(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, const char *val_str, 
1176         ly_bool output, struct lyd_node **node) 
1177 { 
1178     return _lyd_new_term(parent, module, name, val_str, val_str ? strlen(val_str) : 0, LY_VALUE_JSON, output, node); 
1179 }

1279 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1280 lyd_new_meta(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, 
1281         const char *val_str, ly_bool clear_dflt, struct lyd_meta **meta) 
1282 { 
1283     const char *prefix, *tmp; 
1284     size_t pref_len, name_len; 
1285  
1286     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ctx || parent, name, module || strchr(name, ':'), parent || meta, LY_EINVAL); 
1287     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(ctx, parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, module ? module->ctx : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
1288     if (!ctx) { 
1289         ctx = LYD_CTX(parent); 
1290     } 
1291  
1292     if (parent && !parent->schema) { 
1293         LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Cannot add metadata \"%s\" to an opaque node \"%s\".", name, LYD_NAME(parent)); 
1294         return LY_EINVAL; 
1295     } 
1296     if (meta) { 
1297         *meta = NULL; 
1298     } 
1299  
1300     /* parse the name */ 
1301     tmp = name; 
1302     if (ly_parse_nodeid(&tmp, &prefix, &pref_len, &name, &name_len) || tmp[0]) { 
1303         LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Metadata name \"%s\" is not valid.", name); 
1304         return LY_EINVAL; 
1305     } 
1306  
1307     /* find the module */ 
1308     if (prefix) { 
1309         module = ly_ctx_get_module_implemented2(ctx, prefix, pref_len); 
1310         LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!module, LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Module \"%.*s\" not found.", (int)pref_len, prefix), LY_ENOTFOUND); 
1311     } 
1312  
1313     /* set value if none */ 
1314     if (!val_str) { 
1315         val_str = ""; 
1316     } 
1317  
1318     return lyd_create_meta(parent, meta, module, name, name_len, val_str, strlen(val_str), NULL, LY_VALUE_JSON, 
1319             NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, parent ? parent->schema : NULL, clear_dflt, NULL); 
1320 } 
1321  
1322 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1323 lyd_new_meta2(const struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lyd_node *parent, ly_bool clear_dflt, const struct lyd_attr *attr, 
1324         struct lyd_meta **meta) 
1325 { 
1326     const struct lys_module *mod; 
1327  
1328     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, ctx, attr, parent || meta, LY_EINVAL); 
1329     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(ctx, parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
1330  
1331     if (parent && !parent->schema) { 
1332         LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Cannot add metadata to an opaque node \"%s\".", ((struct lyd_node_opaq *)parent)->name); 
1333         return LY_EINVAL; 
1334     } 
1335     if (meta) { 
1336         *meta = NULL; 
1337     } 
1338  
1339     switch (attr->format) { 
1340     case LY_VALUE_XML: 
1341         mod = ly_ctx_get_module_implemented_ns(ctx, attr->name.module_ns); 
1342         if (!mod) { 
1343             LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Module with namespace \"%s\" not found.", attr->name.module_ns); 
1344             return LY_ENOTFOUND; 
1345         } 
1346         break; 
1347     case LY_VALUE_JSON: 
1348         mod = ly_ctx_get_module_implemented(ctx, attr->name.module_name); 
1349         if (!mod) { 
1350             LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Module \"%s\" not found.", attr->name.module_name); 
1351             return LY_ENOTFOUND; 
1352         } 
1353         break; 
1354     default: 
1355         LOGINT_RET(ctx); 
1356     } 
1357  
1358     return lyd_create_meta(parent, meta, mod, attr->name.name, strlen(attr->name.name), attr->value, strlen(attr->value), 
1359             NULL, attr->format, attr->val_prefix_data, attr->hints, parent ? parent->schema : NULL, clear_dflt, NULL); 
1360 }

1362 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1363 lyd_new_opaq(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *name, const char *value, 
1364         const char *prefix, const char *module_name, struct lyd_node **node) 
1365 { 
1366     struct lyd_node *ret = NULL; 
1367  
1368     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, parent || ctx, parent || node, name, module_name, !prefix || !strcmp(prefix, module_name), LY_EINVAL); 
1369     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(ctx, parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
1370  
1371     if (!ctx) { 
1372         ctx = LYD_CTX(parent); 
1373     } 
1374     if (!value) { 
1375         value = ""; 
1376     } 
1377  
1378     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_opaq(ctx, name, strlen(name), prefix, prefix ? strlen(prefix) : 0, module_name, 
1379             strlen(module_name), value, strlen(value), NULL, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, 0, &ret)); 
1380     if (parent) { 
1381         lyd_insert_node(parent, NULL, ret, 1); 
1382     } 
1383  
1384     if (node) { 
1385         *node = ret; 
1386     } 
1387     return LY_SUCCESS; 
1388 }

1400         ctx = LYD_CTX(parent);

1418 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1419 lyd_new_attr(struct lyd_node *parent, const char *module_name, const char *name, const char *value, 
1420         struct lyd_attr **attr) 
1421 { 
1422     struct lyd_attr *ret = NULL; 
1423     const struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
1424     const char *prefix, *tmp; 
1425     size_t pref_len, name_len, mod_len; 
1426  
1427     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, parent, !parent->schema, name, LY_EINVAL); 
1428  
1429     ctx = LYD_CTX(parent); 
1430  
1431     /* parse the name */ 
1432     tmp = name; 
1433     if (ly_parse_nodeid(&tmp, &prefix, &pref_len, &name, &name_len) || tmp[0]) { 
1434         LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Attribute name \"%s\" is not valid.", name); 
1435         return LY_EVALID; 
1436     } 
1437  
1438     if ((pref_len == 3) && !strncmp(prefix, "xml", 3)) { 
1439         /* not a prefix but special name */ 
1440         name = prefix; 
1441         name_len += 1 + pref_len; 
1442         prefix = NULL; 
1443         pref_len = 0; 
1444     } 
1445  
1446     /* get the module */ 
1447     if (module_name) { 
1448         mod_len = strlen(module_name); 
1449     } else { 
1450         module_name = prefix; 
1451         mod_len = pref_len; 
1452     } 
1453  
1454     /* set value if none */ 
1455     if (!value) { 
1456         value = ""; 
1457     } 
1458  
1459     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_attr(parent, &ret, ctx, name, name_len, prefix, pref_len, module_name, mod_len, value, 
1460             strlen(value), NULL, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA)); 
1461  
1462     if (attr) { 
1463         *attr = ret; 
1464     } 
1465     return LY_SUCCESS; 
1466 } 
1467  
1468 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1469 lyd_new_attr2(struct lyd_node *parent, const char *module_ns, const char *name, const char *value, 
1470         struct lyd_attr **attr) 
1471 { 
1472     struct lyd_attr *ret = NULL; 
1473     const struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
1474     const char *prefix, *tmp; 
1475     size_t pref_len, name_len; 
1476  
1477     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, parent, !parent->schema, name, LY_EINVAL); 
1478  
1479     ctx = LYD_CTX(parent); 
1480  
1481     /* parse the name */ 
1482     tmp = name; 
1483     if (ly_parse_nodeid(&tmp, &prefix, &pref_len, &name, &name_len) || tmp[0]) { 
1484         LOGERR(ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Attribute name \"%s\" is not valid.", name); 
1485         return LY_EVALID; 
1486     } 
1487  
1488     if ((pref_len == 3) && !strncmp(prefix, "xml", 3)) { 
1489         /* not a prefix but special name */ 
1490         name = prefix; 
1491         name_len += 1 + pref_len; 
1492         prefix = NULL; 
1493         pref_len = 0; 
1494     } 
1495  
1496     /* set value if none */ 
1497     if (!value) { 
1498         value = ""; 
1499     } 
1500  
1501     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_attr(parent, &ret, ctx, name, name_len, prefix, pref_len, module_ns, 
1502             module_ns ? strlen(module_ns) : 0, value, strlen(value), NULL, LY_VALUE_XML, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA)); 
1503

1505         *attr = ret; 
1506     } 
1507     return LY_SUCCESS; 
1508 } 
1509  
1510 /** 
1511  * @brief Change the value of a term (leaf or leaf-list) node. 
1512  * 
1513  * Node changed this way is always considered explicitly set, meaning its default flag 
1514  * is always cleared. 
1515  * 
1516  * @param[in] term Term node to change. 
1517  * @param[in] value New value to set. 
1518  * @param[in] value_len Length of @p value. 
1519  * @param[in] format Format of @p value. 
1520  * @return LY_SUCCESS if value was changed, 
1521  * @return LY_EEXIST if value was the same and only the default flag was cleared, 
1522  * @return LY_ENOT if the values were equal and no change occured, 
1523  * @return LY_ERR value on other errors. 
1524  */ 
1525 static LY_ERR 
1526 _lyd_change_term(struct lyd_node *term, const void *value, size_t value_len, LY_VALUE_FORMAT format) 
1527 { 
1528     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
1529     struct lysc_type *type; 
1530     struct lyd_node_term *t; 
1531     struct lyd_node *parent; 
1532     struct lyd_value val = {0}; 
1533     ly_bool dflt_change, val_change; 
1534  
1535     assert(term && term->schema && (term->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM)); 
1536  
1537     t = (struct lyd_node_term *)term; 
1538     type = ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)term->schema)->type; 
1539  
1540     /* parse the new value */ 
1541     LOG_LOCSET(term->schema, term, NULL, NULL); 
1542     ret = lyd_value_store(LYD_CTX(term), &val, type, value, value_len, NULL, format, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, term->schema, NULL); 
1543     LOG_LOCBACK(term->schema ? 1 : 0, 1, 0, 0); 
1544     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret, cleanup); 
1545  
1546     /* compare original and new value */ 
1547     if (type->plugin->compare(&t->value, &val)) { 
1548         /* values differ, switch them */ 
1549         type->plugin->free(LYD_CTX(term), &t->value); 
1550         t->value = val; 
1551         memset(&val, 0, sizeof val); 
1552         val_change = 1; 
1553     } else { 
1554         val_change = 0; 
1555     } 
1556  
1557     /* always clear the default flag */ 
1558     if (term->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) { 
1559         for (parent = term; parent; parent = lyd_parent(parent)) { 
1560             parent->flags &= ~LYD_DEFAULT; 
1561         } 
1562         dflt_change = 1; 
1563     } else { 
1564         dflt_change = 0; 
1565     } 
1566  
1567     if (val_change || dflt_change) { 
1568         /* make the node non-validated */ 
1569         term->flags &= LYD_NEW; 
1570     } 
1571  
1572     if (val_change) { 
1573         if (term->schema->nodetype == LYS_LEAFLIST) { 
1574             /* leaf-list needs to be hashed again and re-inserted into parent */ 
1575             lyd_unlink_hash(term); 
1576             lyd_hash(term); 
1577             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_insert_hash(term), cleanup); 
1578         } else if ((term->schema->flags & LYS_KEY) && term->parent) { 
1579             /* list needs to be updated if its key was changed */ 
1580             assert(term->parent->schema->nodetype == LYS_LIST); 
1581             lyd_unlink_hash(lyd_parent(term)); 
1582             lyd_hash(lyd_parent(term)); 
1583             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_insert_hash(lyd_parent(term)), cleanup); 
1584         } /* else leaf that is not a key, its value is not used for its hash so it does not change */ 
1585     } 
1586  
1587     /* retrun value */ 
1588     if (!val_change) { 
1589         if (dflt_change) { 
1590             /* only default flag change */ 
1591             ret = LY_EEXIST; 
1592         } else { 
1593             /* no change */ 
1594             ret = LY_ENOT; 
1595         } 
1596     } /* else value changed, LY_SUCCESS */ 
1597  
1598 cleanup: 
1599     if (val.realtype) { 
1600         type->plugin->free(LYD_CTX(term), &val); 
1601     } 
1602     return ret; 
1603 } 
1604  
1605 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1606 lyd_change_term(struct lyd_node *term, const char *val_str) 
1607 { 
1608     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, term, term->schema, term->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM, LY_EINVAL); 
1609  
1610     return _lyd_change_term(term, val_str, val_str ? strlen(val_str) : 0, LY_VALUE_JSON); 
1611 } 
1612  
1613 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1614 lyd_change_term_bin(struct lyd_node *term, const void *value, size_t value_len) 
1615 { 
1616     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, term, term->schema, term->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM, LY_EINVAL); 
1617  
1618     return _lyd_change_term(term, value, value_len, LY_VALUE_LYB); 
1619 }

1621 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1622 lyd_change_term_canon(struct lyd_node *term, const char *val_str) 
1623 { 
1624     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, term, term->schema, term->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM, LY_EINVAL);

1627 } 
1628  
1629 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
1630 lyd_change_meta(struct lyd_meta *meta, const char *val_str) 
1631 { 
1632     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
1633     struct lyd_meta *m2 = NULL; 
1634     struct lyd_value val; 
1635     ly_bool val_change; 
1636  
1637     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, meta, LY_EINVAL); 
1638  
1639     if (!val_str) { 
1640         val_str = ""; 
1641     } 
1642  
1643     /* parse the new value into a new meta structure */ 
1644     ret = lyd_create_meta(NULL, &m2, meta->annotation->module, meta->name, strlen(meta->name), val_str, strlen(val_str), 
1645             NULL, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, meta->parent ? meta->parent->schema : NULL, 0, NULL); 
1646     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret, cleanup); 
1647  
1648     /* compare original and new value */ 
1649     if (lyd_compare_meta(meta, m2)) { 
1650         /* values differ, switch them */ 
1651         val = meta->value; 
1652         meta->value = m2->value; 
1653         m2->value = val; 
1654         val_change = 1; 
1655     } else { 
1656         val_change = 0; 
1657     } 
1658  
1659     /* retrun value */ 
1660     if (!val_change) { 
1661         /* no change */ 
1662         ret = LY_ENOT; 
1663     } /* else value changed, LY_SUCCESS */ 
1664  
1665 cleanup: 
1666     lyd_free_meta_single(m2); 
1667     return ret; 
1668 } 
1669  
1670 /** 
1671  * @brief Update node value. 
1672  * 
1673  * @param[in] node Node to update. 
1674  * @param[in] value New value to set. 
1675  * @param[in] value_len Length of @p value. 
1676  * @param[in] value_type Type of @p value for anydata/anyxml node. 
1677  * @param[in] format Format of @p value. 
1678  * @param[out] new_parent Set to @p node if the value was updated, otherwise set to NULL. 
1679  * @param[out] new_node Set to @p node if the value was updated, otherwise set to NULL. 
1680  * @return LY_ERR value. 
1681  */ 
1682 static LY_ERR 
1683 lyd_new_path_update(struct lyd_node *node, const void *value, size_t value_len, LYD_ANYDATA_VALUETYPE value_type, 
1684         LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, struct lyd_node **new_parent, struct lyd_node **new_node) 
1685 { 
1686     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
1687     struct lyd_node *new_any; 
1688  
1689     switch (node->schema->nodetype) { 
1690     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
1691     case LYS_NOTIF: 
1692     case LYS_RPC: 
1693     case LYS_ACTION: 
1694     case LYS_LIST: 
1695         /* if it exists, there is nothing to update */ 
1696         *new_parent = NULL; 
1697         *new_node = NULL; 
1698         break; 
1699     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
1700         if (!lysc_is_dup_inst_list(node->schema)) { 
1701             /* if it exists, there is nothing to update */ 
1702             *new_parent = NULL; 
1703             *new_node = NULL; 
1704             break; 
1705         } 
1706     /* fallthrough */ 
1707     case LYS_LEAF: 
1708         ret = _lyd_change_term(node, value, value_len, format); 
1709         if ((ret == LY_SUCCESS) || (ret == LY_EEXIST)) { 
1710             /* there was an actual change (at least of the default flag) */ 
1711             *new_parent = node; 
1712             *new_node = node; 
1713             ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
1714         } else if (ret == LY_ENOT) { 
1715             /* no change */ 
1716             *new_parent = NULL; 
1717             *new_node = NULL; 
1718             ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
1719         } /* else error */ 
1720         break; 
1721     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
1722     case LYS_ANYXML: 
1723         /* create a new any node */ 
1724         LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_any(node->schema, value, value_type, 0, &new_any)); 
1725  
1726         /* compare with the existing one */ 
1727         if (lyd_compare_single(node, new_any, 0)) { 
1728             /* not equal, switch values (so that we can use generic node free) */ 
1729             ((struct lyd_node_any *)new_any)->value = ((struct lyd_node_any *)node)->value; 
1730             ((struct lyd_node_any *)new_any)->value_type = ((struct lyd_node_any *)node)->value_type; 
1731             ((struct lyd_node_any *)node)->value.str = value; 
1732             ((struct lyd_node_any *)node)->value_type = value_type; 
1733  
1734             *new_parent = node; 
1735             *new_node = node; 
1736         } else { 
1737             /* they are equal */ 
1738             *new_parent = NULL; 
1739             *new_node = NULL; 
1740         } 
1741         lyd_free_tree(new_any); 
1742         break; 
1743     default: 
1744         LOGINT(LYD_CTX(node)); 
1745         ret = LY_EINT; 
1746         break; 
1747     } 
1748  
1749     return ret; 
1750 } 
1751  
1752 static LY_ERR 
1753 lyd_new_path_check_find_lypath(struct ly_path *path, const char *str_path, const char *value, size_t value_len, 
1754         LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, uint32_t options) 
1755 { 
1756     LY_ERR r; 
1757     struct ly_path_predicate *pred; 
1758     struct lyd_value val; 
1759     const struct lysc_node *schema = NULL; 
1760     LY_ARRAY_COUNT_TYPE u, new_count; 
1761     int create = 0; 
1762  
1763     assert(path); 
1764  
1765     /* go through all the compiled nodes */ 
1766     LY_ARRAY_FOR(path, u) { 
1767         schema = path[u].node; 
1768  
1769         if (lysc_is_dup_inst_list(schema)) { 
1770             if (path[u].pred_type == LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_NONE) { 
1771                 /* creating a new key-less list or state leaf-list instance */ 
1772                 create = 1; 
1773                 new_count = u; 
1774             } else if (path[u].pred_type != LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_POSITION) { 
1775                 LOG_LOCSET(schema, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
1776                 LOGVAL(schema->module->ctx, LYVE_XPATH, "Invalid predicate for %s \"%s\" in path \"%s\".", 
1777                         lys_nodetype2str(schema->nodetype), schema->name, str_path); 
1778                 LOG_LOCBACK(1, 0, 0, 0); 
1779                 return LY_EINVAL; 
1780             } 
1781         } else if ((schema->nodetype == LYS_LIST) && (path[u].pred_type != LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_LIST)) { 
1782             if ((u < LY_ARRAY_COUNT(path) - 1) || !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_OPAQ)) { 
1783                 LOG_LOCSET(schema, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
1784                 LOGVAL(schema->module->ctx, LYVE_XPATH, "Predicate missing for %s \"%s\" in path \"%s\".", 
1785                         lys_nodetype2str(schema->nodetype), schema->name, str_path); 
1786                 LOG_LOCBACK(1, 0, 0, 0); 
1787                 return LY_EINVAL; 
1788             } /* else creating an opaque list */ 
1789         } else if ((schema->nodetype == LYS_LEAFLIST) && (path[u].pred_type != LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_LEAFLIST)) { 
1790             r = LY_SUCCESS; 
1791             if (options & LYD_NEW_PATH_OPAQ) { 
1792                 r = lyd_value_validate(NULL, schema, value, value_len, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
1793             } 
1794             if (!r) { 
1795                 /* try to store the value */ 
1796                 LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_value_store(schema->module->ctx, &val, ((struct lysc_node_leaflist *)schema)->type, 
1797                         value, value_len, NULL, format, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, schema, NULL)); 
1798                 ++((struct lysc_type *)val.realtype)->refcount; 
1799  
1800                 /* store the new predicate so that it is used when searching for this instance */ 
1801                 path[u].pred_type = LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_LEAFLIST; 
1802                 LY_ARRAY_NEW_RET(schema->module->ctx, path[u].predicates, pred, LY_EMEM); 
1803                 pred->value = val; 
1804             } /* else we have opaq flag and the value is not valid, leave no predicate and then create an opaque node */ 
1805         } 
1806     } 
1807  
1808     if (create) { 
1809         /* hide the nodes that should always be created so they are not found */ 
1810         while (new_count < LY_ARRAY_COUNT(path)) { 
1811             LY_ARRAY_DECREMENT(path); 
1812         } 
1813     } 
1814  
1815     return LY_SUCCESS; 
1816 } 
1817  
1818 /** 
1819  * @brief Create a new node in the data tree based on a path. All node types can be created. 
1820  * 
1821  * If @p path points to a list key, the key value from the predicate is used and @p value is ignored. 
1822  * Also, if a leaf-list is being created and both a predicate is defined in @p path 
1823  * and @p value is set, the predicate is preferred. 
1824  * 
1825  * For key-less lists and state leaf-lists, positional predicates can be used. If no preciate is used for these 
1826  * nodes, they are always created. 
1827  * 
1828  * @param[in] parent Data parent to add to/modify, can be NULL. Note that in case a first top-level sibling is used, 
1829  * it may no longer be first if @p path is absolute and starts with a non-existing top-level node inserted 
1830  * before @p parent. Use ::lyd_first_sibling() to adjust @p parent in these cases. 
1831  * @param[in] ctx libyang context, must be set if @p parent is NULL. 
1832  * @param[in] ext Extension instance where the node being created is defined. This argument takes effect only for absolute 
1833  * path or when the relative paths touches document root (top-level). In such cases the present extension instance replaces 
1834  * searching for the appropriate module. 
1835  * @param[in] path [Path](@ref howtoXPath) to create. 
1836  * @param[in] value Value of the new leaf/leaf-list (const char *) in ::LY_VALUE_JSON format. If creating an 
1837  * anyxml/anydata node, the expected type depends on @p value_type. For other node types, it should be NULL. 
1838  * @param[in] value_len Length of @p value in bytes. May be 0 if @p value is a zero-terminated string. Ignored when 
1839  * creating anyxml/anydata nodes. 
1840  * @param[in] value_type Anyxml/anydata node @p value type. 
1841  * @param[in] options Bitmask of options, see @ref pathoptions. 
1842  * @param[out] new_parent Optional first parent node created. If only one node was created, equals to @p new_node. 
1843  * @param[out] new_node Optional last node created. 
1844  * @return LY_ERR value. 
1845  */ 
1846 static LY_ERR 
1847 lyd_new_path_(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, const char *path, 
1848         const void *value, size_t value_len, LYD_ANYDATA_VALUETYPE value_type, uint32_t options, 
1849         struct lyd_node **new_parent, struct lyd_node **new_node) 
1850 { 
1851     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS, r; 
1852     struct lyxp_expr *exp = NULL; 
1853     struct ly_path *p = NULL; 
1854     struct lyd_node *nparent = NULL, *nnode = NULL, *node = NULL, *cur_parent; 
1855     const struct lysc_node *schema; 
1856     const struct lyd_value *val = NULL; 
1857     LY_ARRAY_COUNT_TYPE path_idx = 0, orig_count = 0; 
1858     LY_VALUE_FORMAT format; 
1859  
1860     assert(parent || ctx); 
1861     assert(path && ((path[0] == '/') || parent)); 
1862     assert(!(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_BIN_VALUE) || !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_CANON_VALUE)); 
1863  
1864     if (!ctx) { 
1865         ctx = LYD_CTX(parent); 
1866     } 
1867     if (value && !value_len) { 
1868         value_len = strlen(value); 
1869     } 
1870     if (options & LYD_NEW_PATH_BIN_VALUE) { 
1871         format = LY_VALUE_LYB; 
1872     } else if (options & LYD_NEW_PATH_CANON_VALUE) { 
1873         format = LY_VALUE_CANON; 
1874     } else { 
1875         format = LY_VALUE_JSON; 
1876     } 
1877  
1878     /* parse path */ 
1879     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = ly_path_parse(ctx, NULL, path, strlen(path), 0, LY_PATH_BEGIN_EITHER, LY_PATH_PREFIX_OPTIONAL, 
1880             LY_PATH_PRED_SIMPLE, &exp), cleanup); 
1881  
1882     /* compile path */ 
1883     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = ly_path_compile(ctx, NULL, lyd_node_schema(parent), ext, exp, options & LYD_NEW_PATH_OUTPUT ? 
1884             LY_PATH_OPER_OUTPUT : LY_PATH_OPER_INPUT, LY_PATH_TARGET_MANY, 0, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, &p), cleanup); 
1885  
1886     /* check the compiled path before searching existing nodes, it may be shortened */ 
1887     orig_count = LY_ARRAY_COUNT(p); 
1888     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_new_path_check_find_lypath(p, path, value, value_len, format, options), cleanup); 
1889  
1890     /* try to find any existing nodes in the path */ 
1891     if (parent) { 
1892         ret = ly_path_eval_partial(p, parent, &path_idx, &node); 
1893         if (ret == LY_SUCCESS) { 
1894             if (orig_count == LY_ARRAY_COUNT(p)) { 
1895                 /* the node exists, are we supposed to update it or is it just a default? */ 
1896                 if (!(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_UPDATE) && !(node->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
1897                     LOG_LOCSET(NULL, node, NULL, NULL); 
1898                     LOGVAL(ctx, LYVE_REFERENCE, "Path \"%s\" already exists", path); 
1899                     LOG_LOCBACK(0, 1, 0, 0); 
1900                     ret = LY_EEXIST; 
1901                     goto cleanup; 
1902                 } 
1903  
1904                 /* update the existing node */ 
1905                 ret = lyd_new_path_update(node, value, value_len, value_type, format, &nparent, &nnode); 
1906                 goto cleanup; 
1907             } /* else we were not searching for the whole path */ 
1908         } else if (ret == LY_EINCOMPLETE) { 
1909             /* some nodes were found, adjust the iterator to the next segment */ 
1910             ++path_idx; 
1911         } else if (ret == LY_ENOTFOUND) { 
1912             /* we will create the nodes from top-level, default behavior (absolute path), or from the parent (relative path) */ 
1913             if (lysc_data_parent(p[0].node)) { 
1914                 node = parent; 
1915             } 
1916         } else { 
1917             /* error */ 
1918             goto cleanup; 
1919         } 
1920     } 
1921  
1922     /* restore the full path for creating new nodes */ 
1923     while (orig_count > LY_ARRAY_COUNT(p)) { 
1924         LY_ARRAY_INCREMENT(p); 
1925     } 
1926  
1927     /* create all the non-existing nodes in a loop */ 
1928     for ( ; path_idx < LY_ARRAY_COUNT(p); ++path_idx) { 
1929         cur_parent = node; 
1930         schema = p[path_idx].node; 
1931  
1932         switch (schema->nodetype) { 
1933         case LYS_LIST: 
1934             if (lysc_is_dup_inst_list(schema)) { 
1935                 /* create key-less list instance */ 
1936                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_inner(schema, &node), cleanup); 
1937             } else if ((options & LYD_NEW_PATH_OPAQ) && (p[path_idx].pred_type == LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_NONE)) { 
1938                 /* creating opaque list without keys */ 
1939                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_opaq(ctx, schema->name, strlen(schema->name), NULL, 0, 
1940                         schema->module->name, strlen(schema->module->name), NULL, 0, NULL, LY_VALUE_JSON, NULL, 
1941                         LYD_NODEHINT_LIST, &node), cleanup); 
1942             } else { 
1943                 /* create standard list instance */ 
1944                 assert(p[path_idx].pred_type == LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_LIST); 
1945                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_list(schema, p[path_idx].predicates, &node), cleanup); 
1946             } 
1947             break; 
1948         case LYS_CONTAINER: 
1949         case LYS_NOTIF: 
1950         case LYS_RPC: 
1951         case LYS_ACTION: 
1952             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_inner(schema, &node), cleanup); 
1953             break; 
1954         case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
1955             if ((options & LYD_NEW_PATH_OPAQ) && (p[path_idx].pred_type != LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_LEAFLIST)) { 
1956                 /* we have not checked this only for dup-inst lists, otherwise it must be opaque */ 
1957                 r = LY_EVALID; 
1958                 if (lysc_is_dup_inst_list(schema)) { 
1959                     /* validate value */ 
1960                     r = lyd_value_validate(NULL, schema, value ? value : "", value_len, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
1961                 } 
1962                 if (r && (r != LY_EINCOMPLETE)) { 
1963                     /* creating opaque leaf-list */ 
1964                     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_opaq(ctx, schema->name, strlen(schema->name), value, value_len, 
1965                             schema->module->name, strlen(schema->module->name), NULL, 0, NULL, format, NULL, 
1966                             LYD_NODEHINT_LEAFLIST, &node), cleanup); 
1967                     break; 
1968                 } 
1969             } 
1970  
1971             /* get value to set */ 
1972             if (p[path_idx].pred_type == LY_PATH_PREDTYPE_LEAFLIST) { 
1973                 val = &p[path_idx].predicates[0].value; 
1974             } 
1975  
1976             /* create a leaf-list instance */ 
1977             if (val) { 
1978                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_term2(schema, &p[path_idx].predicates[0].value, &node), cleanup); 
1979             } else { 
1980                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_term(schema, value, value_len, NULL, format, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, 
1981                         NULL, &node), cleanup); 
1982             } 
1983             break; 
1984         case LYS_LEAF: 
1985             if (lysc_is_key(schema) && cur_parent->schema) { 
1986                 /* it must have been already created or some error will occur later */ 
1987                 lyd_find_sibling_schema(lyd_child(cur_parent), schema, &node); 
1988                 assert(node); 
1989                 goto next_iter; 
1990             } 
1991  
1992             if (options & LYD_NEW_PATH_OPAQ) { 
1993                 if (cur_parent && !cur_parent->schema) { 
1994                     /* always create opaque nodes for opaque parents */ 
1995                     r = LY_ENOT; 
1996                 } else { 
1997                     /* validate value */ 
1998                     r = lyd_value_validate(NULL, schema, value ? value : "", value_len, NULL, NULL, NULL); 
1999                 } 
2000                 if (r && (r != LY_EINCOMPLETE)) { 
2001                     /* creating opaque leaf */ 
2002                     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_opaq(ctx, schema->name, strlen(schema->name), value, value_len, 
2003                             schema->module->name, strlen(schema->module->name), NULL, 0, NULL, format, NULL, 0, &node), 
2004                             cleanup); 
2005                     break; 
2006                 } 
2007             } 
2008  
2009             /* create a leaf instance */ 
2010             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_term(schema, value, value_len, NULL, format, NULL, LYD_HINT_DATA, NULL, 
2011                     &node), cleanup); 
2012             break; 
2013         case LYS_ANYDATA: 
2014         case LYS_ANYXML: 
2015             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_create_any(schema, value, value_type, 0, &node), cleanup); 
2016             break; 
2017         default: 
2018             LOGINT(ctx); 
2019             ret = LY_EINT; 
2020             goto cleanup; 
2021         } 
2022  
2023         if (cur_parent) { 
2024             /* connect to the parent */ 
2025             lyd_insert_node(cur_parent, NULL, node, 0); 
2026         } else if (parent) { 
2027             /* connect to top-level siblings */ 
2028             lyd_insert_node(NULL, &parent, node, 0); 
2029         } 
2030  
2031 next_iter: 
2032         /* update remembered nodes */ 
2033         if (!nparent) { 
2034             nparent = node; 
2035         } 
2036         nnode = node; 
2037     } 
2038  
2039 cleanup: 
2040     lyxp_expr_free(ctx, exp); 
2041     if (p) { 
2042         while (orig_count > LY_ARRAY_COUNT(p)) { 
2043             LY_ARRAY_INCREMENT(p); 
2044         } 
2045     } 
2046     ly_path_free(ctx, p); 
2047     if (!ret) { 
2048         /* set out params only on success */ 
2049         if (new_parent) { 
2050             *new_parent = nparent; 
2051         } 
2052         if (new_node) { 
2053             *new_node = nnode; 
2054         } 
2055     } else { 
2056         lyd_free_tree(nparent); 
2057     } 
2058     return ret; 
2059 } 
2060  
2061 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2062 lyd_new_path(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *path, const char *value, uint32_t options, 
2063         struct lyd_node **node) 
2064 { 
2065     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, parent || ctx, path, (path[0] == '/') || parent, 
2066             !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_BIN_VALUE) || !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_CANON_VALUE), LY_EINVAL); 
2067     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, ctx, LY_EINVAL); 
2068  
2069     return lyd_new_path_(parent, ctx, NULL, path, value, 0, LYD_ANYDATA_STRING, options, node, NULL); 
2070 } 
2071  
2072 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2073 lyd_new_path2(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *path, const void *value, 
2074         size_t value_len, LYD_ANYDATA_VALUETYPE value_type, uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **new_parent, 
2075         struct lyd_node **new_node) 
2076 { 
2077     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, parent || ctx, path, (path[0] == '/') || parent, 
2078             !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_BIN_VALUE) || !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_CANON_VALUE), LY_EINVAL); 
2079     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, ctx, LY_EINVAL); 
2080  
2081     return lyd_new_path_(parent, ctx, NULL, path, value, value_len, value_type, options, new_parent, new_node); 
2082 } 
2083  
2084 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2085 lyd_new_ext_path(struct lyd_node *parent, const struct lysc_ext_instance *ext, const char *path, const void *value, 
2086         uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **node) 
2087 { 
2088     const struct ly_ctx *ctx = ext ? ext->module->ctx : NULL; 
2089  
2090     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, ext, path, (path[0] == '/') || parent, 
2091             !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_BIN_VALUE) || !(options & LYD_NEW_PATH_CANON_VALUE), LY_EINVAL); 
2092     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(parent ? LYD_CTX(parent) : NULL, ctx, LY_EINVAL); 
2093  
2094     return lyd_new_path_(parent, ctx, ext, path, value, 0, LYD_ANYDATA_STRING, options, node, NULL); 
2095 } 
2096  
2097 LY_ERR 
2098 lyd_new_implicit_r(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_node **first, const struct lysc_node *sparent, 
2099         const struct lys_module *mod, struct ly_set *node_when, struct ly_set *node_types, uint32_t impl_opts, 
2100         struct lyd_node **diff) 
2101 { 
2102     LY_ERR ret; 
2103     const struct lysc_node *iter = NULL; 
2104     struct lyd_node *node = NULL; 
2105     struct lyd_value **dflts; 
2106     LY_ARRAY_COUNT_TYPE u; 
2107     uint32_t getnext_opts; 
2108  
2109     assert(first && (parent || sparent || mod)); 
2110  
2111     if (!sparent && parent) { 
2112         sparent = parent->schema; 
2113     } 
2114  
2115     getnext_opts = LYS_GETNEXT_WITHCHOICE; 
2116     if (impl_opts & LYD_IMPLICIT_OUTPUT) { 
2117         getnext_opts |= LYS_GETNEXT_OUTPUT; 
2118     } 
2119  
2120     while ((iter = lys_getnext(iter, sparent, mod ? mod->compiled : NULL, getnext_opts))) { 
2121         if ((impl_opts & LYD_IMPLICIT_NO_STATE) && (iter->flags & LYS_CONFIG_R)) { 
2122             continue; 
2123         } else if ((impl_opts & LYD_IMPLICIT_NO_CONFIG) && (iter->flags & LYS_CONFIG_W)) { 
2124             continue; 
2125         } 
2126  
2127         switch (iter->nodetype) { 
2128         case LYS_CHOICE: 
2129             node = lys_getnext_data(NULL, *first, NULL, iter, NULL); 
2130             if (!node && ((struct lysc_node_choice *)iter)->dflt) { 
2131                 /* create default case data */ 
2132                 LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_new_implicit_r(parent, first, &((struct lysc_node_choice *)iter)->dflt->node, 
2133                         NULL, node_when, node_types, impl_opts, diff)); 
2134             } else if (node) { 
2135                 /* create any default data in the existing case */ 
2136                 assert(node->schema->parent->nodetype == LYS_CASE); 
2137                 LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_new_implicit_r(parent, first, node->schema->parent, NULL, node_when, node_types, 
2138                         impl_opts, diff)); 
2139             } 
2140             break; 
2141         case LYS_CONTAINER: 
2142             if (!(iter->flags & LYS_PRESENCE) && lyd_find_sibling_val(*first, iter, NULL, 0, NULL)) { 
2143                 /* create default NP container */ 
2144                 LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_create_inner(iter, &node)); 
2145                 node->flags = LYD_DEFAULT | (lysc_has_when(iter) ? LYD_WHEN_TRUE : 0); 
2146                 lyd_insert_node(parent, first, node, 0); 
2147  
2148                 if (lysc_has_when(iter) && node_when) { 
2149                     /* remember to resolve when */ 
2150                     LY_CHECK_RET(ly_set_add(node_when, node, 1, NULL)); 
2151                 } 
2152                 if (diff) { 
2153                     /* add into diff */ 
2154                     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_val_diff_add(node, LYD_DIFF_OP_CREATE, diff)); 
2155                 } 
2156  
2157                 /* create any default children */ 
2158                 LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_new_implicit_r(node, lyd_node_child_p(node), NULL, NULL, node_when, node_types, 
2159                         impl_opts, diff)); 
2160             } 
2161             break; 
2162         case LYS_LEAF: 
2163             if (!(impl_opts & LYD_IMPLICIT_NO_DEFAULTS) && ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)iter)->dflt && 
2164                     lyd_find_sibling_val(*first, iter, NULL, 0, NULL)) { 
2165                 /* create default leaf */ 
2166                 ret = lyd_create_term2(iter, ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)iter)->dflt, &node); 
2167                 if (ret == LY_EINCOMPLETE) { 
2168                     if (node_types) { 
2169                         /* remember to resolve type */ 
2170                         LY_CHECK_RET(ly_set_add(node_types, node, 1, NULL)); 
2171                     }
