2172                 } else if (ret) { 
2173                     return ret; 
2174                 } 
2175                 node->flags = LYD_DEFAULT | (lysc_has_when(iter) ? LYD_WHEN_TRUE : 0); 
2176                 lyd_insert_node(parent, first, node, 0); 
2177  
2178                 if (lysc_has_when(iter) && node_when) { 
2179                     /* remember to resolve when */ 
2180                     LY_CHECK_RET(ly_set_add(node_when, node, 1, NULL)); 
2181                 } 
2182                 if (diff) { 
2183                     /* add into diff */ 
2184                     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_val_diff_add(node, LYD_DIFF_OP_CREATE, diff)); 
2185                 } 
2186             } 
2187             break; 
2188         case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
2189             if (!(impl_opts & LYD_IMPLICIT_NO_DEFAULTS) && ((struct lysc_node_leaflist *)iter)->dflts && 
2190                     lyd_find_sibling_val(*first, iter, NULL, 0, NULL)) { 
2191                 /* create all default leaf-lists */ 
2192                 dflts = ((struct lysc_node_leaflist *)iter)->dflts; 
2193                 LY_ARRAY_FOR(dflts, u) { 
2194                     ret = lyd_create_term2(iter, dflts[u], &node); 
2195                     if (ret == LY_EINCOMPLETE) { 
2196                         if (node_types) { 
2197                             /* remember to resolve type */ 
2198                             LY_CHECK_RET(ly_set_add(node_types, node, 1, NULL)); 
2199                         } 
2200                     } else if (ret) { 
2201                         return ret; 
2202                     } 
2203                     node->flags = LYD_DEFAULT | (lysc_has_when(iter) ? LYD_WHEN_TRUE : 0); 
2204                     lyd_insert_node(parent, first, node, 0); 
2205  
2206                     if (lysc_has_when(iter) && node_when) { 
2207                         /* remember to resolve when */ 
2208                         LY_CHECK_RET(ly_set_add(node_when, node, 1, NULL)); 
2209                     } 
2210                     if (diff) { 
2211                         /* add into diff */ 
2212                         LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_val_diff_add(node, LYD_DIFF_OP_CREATE, diff)); 
2213                     } 
2214                 } 
2215             } 
2216             break; 
2217         default: 
2218             /* without defaults */ 
2219             break; 
2220         } 
2221     } 
2222  
2223     return LY_SUCCESS; 
2224 } 
2225  
2226 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2227 lyd_new_implicit_tree(struct lyd_node *tree, uint32_t implicit_options, struct lyd_node **diff) 
2228 { 
2229     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
2230     struct lyd_node *node; 
2231     struct ly_set node_when = {0}; 
2232  
2233     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, tree, LY_EINVAL); 
2234     if (diff) { 
2235         *diff = NULL; 
2236     } 
2237  
2238     LYD_TREE_DFS_BEGIN(tree, node) { 
2239         /* skip added default nodes */ 
2240         if (((node->flags & (LYD_DEFAULT | LYD_NEW)) != (LYD_DEFAULT | LYD_NEW)) && 
2241                 (node->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_INNER)) { 
2242             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_new_implicit_r(node, lyd_node_child_p(node), NULL, NULL, &node_when, NULL, 
2243                     implicit_options, diff), cleanup); 
2244         } 
2245  
2246         LYD_TREE_DFS_END(tree, node); 
2247     } 
2248  
2249     /* resolve when and remove any invalid defaults */ 
2250     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_validate_unres(&tree, NULL, &node_when, LYXP_IGNORE_WHEN, NULL, NULL, NULL, 0, diff), cleanup); 
2251  
2252 cleanup: 
2253     ly_set_erase(&node_when, NULL); 
2254     if (ret && diff) { 
2255         lyd_free_all(*diff); 
2256         *diff = NULL; 
2257     } 
2258     return ret; 
2259 } 
2260  
2261 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2262 lyd_new_implicit_all(struct lyd_node **tree, const struct ly_ctx *ctx, uint32_t implicit_options, struct lyd_node **diff) 
2263 { 
2264     const struct lys_module *mod; 
2265     struct lyd_node *d = NULL; 
2266     uint32_t i = 0; 
2267     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
2268  
2269     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(ctx, tree, *tree || ctx, LY_EINVAL); 
2270     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(*tree ? LYD_CTX(*tree) : NULL, ctx, LY_EINVAL); 
2271     if (diff) { 
2272         *diff = NULL; 
2273     } 
2274     if (!ctx) { 
2275         ctx = LYD_CTX(*tree); 
2276     } 
2277  
2278     /* add nodes for each module one-by-one */ 
2279     while ((mod = ly_ctx_get_module_iter(ctx, &i))) { 
2280         if (!mod->implemented) { 
2281             continue; 
2282         } 
2283  
2284         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_new_implicit_module(tree, mod, implicit_options, diff ? &d : NULL), cleanup); 
2285         if (d) { 
2286             /* merge into one diff */ 
2287             lyd_insert_sibling(*diff, d, diff); 
2288  
2289             d = NULL; 
2290         } 
2291     } 
2292  
2293 cleanup: 
2294     if (ret && diff) { 
2295         lyd_free_all(*diff); 
2296         *diff = NULL; 
2297     } 
2298     return ret; 
2299 } 
2300  
2301 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2302 lyd_new_implicit_module(struct lyd_node **tree, const struct lys_module *module, uint32_t implicit_options, 
2303         struct lyd_node **diff) 
2304 { 
2305     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
2306     struct lyd_node *root, *d = NULL; 
2307     struct ly_set node_when = {0}; 
2308  
2309     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, tree, module, LY_EINVAL); 
2310     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(*tree ? LYD_CTX(*tree) : NULL, module ? module->ctx : NULL, LY_EINVAL); 
2311     if (diff) { 
2312         *diff = NULL; 
2313     } 
2314  
2315     /* add all top-level defaults for this module */ 
2316     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_new_implicit_r(NULL, tree, NULL, module, &node_when, NULL, implicit_options, diff), cleanup); 
2317  
2318     /* resolve when and remove any invalid defaults */ 
2319     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_validate_unres(tree, module, &node_when, LYXP_IGNORE_WHEN, NULL, NULL, NULL, 0, diff), 
2320             cleanup); 
2321  
2322     /* process nested nodes */ 
2323     LY_LIST_FOR(*tree, root) { 
2324         /* skip added default nodes */ 
2325         if ((root->flags & (LYD_DEFAULT | LYD_NEW)) != (LYD_DEFAULT | LYD_NEW)) { 
2326             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_new_implicit_tree(root, implicit_options, diff ? &d : NULL), cleanup); 
2327  
2328             if (d) { 
2329                 /* merge into one diff */ 
2330                 lyd_insert_sibling(*diff, d, diff); 
2331  
2332                 d = NULL; 
2333             } 
2334         } 
2335     } 
2336  
2337 cleanup: 
2338     ly_set_erase(&node_when, NULL); 
2339     if (ret && diff) { 
2340         lyd_free_all(*diff); 
2341         *diff = NULL; 
2342     } 
2343     return ret; 
2344 } 
2345  
2346 struct lyd_node * 
2347 lyd_insert_get_next_anchor(const struct lyd_node *first_sibling, const struct lyd_node *new_node) 
2348 { 
2349     const struct lysc_node *schema, *sparent; 
2350     struct lyd_node *match = NULL; 
2351     ly_bool found; 
2352     uint32_t getnext_opts; 
2353  
2354     assert(new_node); 
2355  
2356     if (!first_sibling || !new_node->schema) { 
2357         /* insert at the end, no next anchor */ 
2358         return NULL; 
2359     } 
2360  
2361     assert(!first_sibling || (LYD_CTX(first_sibling) == LYD_CTX(new_node))); 
2362  
2363     getnext_opts = 0; 
2364     if (new_node->schema->flags & LYS_IS_OUTPUT) { 
2365         getnext_opts = LYS_GETNEXT_OUTPUT; 
2366     } 
2367  
2368     if (first_sibling->parent && first_sibling->parent->schema && first_sibling->parent->children_ht) { 
2369         /* find the anchor using hashes */ 
2370         sparent = first_sibling->parent->schema; 
2371         schema = lys_getnext(new_node->schema, sparent, NULL, getnext_opts); 
2372         while (schema) { 
2373             /* keep trying to find the first existing instance of the closest following schema sibling, 
2374              * otherwise return NULL - inserting at the end */ 
2375             if (!lyd_find_sibling_schema(first_sibling, schema, &match)) { 
2376                 break; 
2377             } 
2378  
2379             schema = lys_getnext(schema, sparent, NULL, getnext_opts); 
2380         } 
2381     } else { 
2382         /* find the anchor without hashes */ 
2383         match = (struct lyd_node *)first_sibling; 
2384         sparent = lysc_data_parent(new_node->schema); 
2385         if (!sparent) { 
2386             /* we are in top-level, skip all the data from preceding modules */ 
2387             LY_LIST_FOR(match, match) { 
2388                 if (!match->schema || (strcmp(lyd_owner_module(match)->name, lyd_owner_module(new_node)->name) >= 0)) { 
2389                     break; 
2390                 } 
2391             } 
2392         } 
2393  
2394         /* get the first schema sibling */ 
2395         schema = lys_getnext(NULL, sparent, new_node->schema->module->compiled, getnext_opts); 
2396  
2397         found = 0; 
2398         LY_LIST_FOR(match, match) { 
2399             if (!match->schema || (lyd_owner_module(match) != lyd_owner_module(new_node))) { 
2400                 /* we have found an opaque node, which must be at the end, so use it OR 
2401                  * modules do not match, so we must have traversed all the data from new_node module (if any), 
2402                  * we have found the first node of the next module, that is what we want */ 
2403                 break; 
2404             } 
2405  
2406             /* skip schema nodes until we find the instantiated one */ 
2407             while (!found) { 
2408                 if (new_node->schema == schema) { 
2409                     /* we have found the schema of the new node, continue search to find the first 
2410                      * data node with a different schema (after our schema) */ 
2411                     found = 1; 
2412                     break; 
2413                 } 
2414                 if (match->schema == schema) { 
2415                     /* current node (match) is a data node still before the new node, continue search in data */ 
2416                     break; 
2417                 } 
2418                 schema = lys_getnext(schema, sparent, new_node->schema->module->compiled, getnext_opts); 
2419                 assert(schema); 
2420             } 
2421  
2422             if (found && (match->schema != new_node->schema)) { 
2423                 /* find the next node after we have found our node schema data instance */ 
2424                 break; 
2425             } 
2426         } 
2427     } 
2428  
2429     return match; 
2430 } 
2431  
2432 /** 
2433  * @brief Insert node after a sibling. 
2434  * 
2435  * Handles inserting into NP containers and key-less lists. 
2436  * 
2437  * @param[in] sibling Sibling to insert after. 
2438  * @param[in] node Node to insert. 
2439  */ 
2440 static void 
2441 lyd_insert_after_node(struct lyd_node *sibling, struct lyd_node *node) 
2442 { 
2443     struct lyd_node_inner *par; 
2444  
2445     assert(!node->next && (node->prev == node)); 
2446  
2447     node->next = sibling->next; 
2448     node->prev = sibling; 
2449     sibling->next = node; 
2450     if (node->next) { 
2451         /* sibling had a succeeding node */ 
2452         node->next->prev = node; 
2453     } else { 
2454         /* sibling was last, find first sibling and change its prev */ 
2455         if (sibling->parent) { 
2456             sibling = sibling->parent->child; 
2457         } else { 
2458             for ( ; sibling->prev->next != node; sibling = sibling->prev) {} 
2459         } 
2460         sibling->prev = node; 
2461     } 
2462     node->parent = sibling->parent; 
2463  
2464     for (par = node->parent; par; par = par->parent) { 
2465         if ((par->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) && !(node->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
2466             /* remove default flags from NP containers */ 
2467             par->flags &= ~LYD_DEFAULT; 
2468         } 
2469     } 
2470 } 
2471  
2472 /** 
2473  * @brief Insert node before a sibling. 
2474  * 
2475  * Handles inserting into NP containers and key-less lists. 
2476  * 
2477  * @param[in] sibling Sibling to insert before. 
2478  * @param[in] node Node to insert. 
2479  */ 
2480 static void 
2481 lyd_insert_before_node(struct lyd_node *sibling, struct lyd_node *node) 
2482 { 
2483     struct lyd_node_inner *par; 
2484  
2485     assert(!node->next && (node->prev == node)); 
2486  
2487     node->next = sibling; 
2488     /* covers situation of sibling being first */ 
2489     node->prev = sibling->prev; 
2490     sibling->prev = node; 
2491     if (node->prev->next) { 
2492         /* sibling had a preceding node */ 
2493         node->prev->next = node; 
2494     } else if (sibling->parent) { 
2495         /* sibling was first and we must also change parent child pointer */ 
2496         sibling->parent->child = node; 
2497     } 
2498     node->parent = sibling->parent; 
2499  
2500     for (par = node->parent; par; par = par->parent) { 
2501         if ((par->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) && !(node->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
2502             /* remove default flags from NP containers */ 
2503             par->flags &= ~LYD_DEFAULT; 
2504         } 
2505     } 
2506 } 
2507  
2508 /** 
2509  * @brief Insert node as the first and only child of a parent. 
2510  * 
2511  * Handles inserting into NP containers and key-less lists. 
2512  * 
2513  * @param[in] parent Parent to insert into. 
2514  * @param[in] node Node to insert. 
2515  */ 
2516 static void 
2517 lyd_insert_only_child(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_node *node) 
2518 { 
2519     struct lyd_node_inner *par; 
2520  
2521     assert(parent && !lyd_child(parent) && !node->next && (node->prev == node)); 
2522     assert(!parent->schema || (parent->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_INNER)); 
2523  
2524     par = (struct lyd_node_inner *)parent; 
2525  
2526     par->child = node; 
2527     node->parent = par; 
2528  
2529     for ( ; par; par = par->parent) { 
2530         if ((par->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) && !(node->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
2531             /* remove default flags from NP containers */ 
2532             par->flags &= ~LYD_DEFAULT; 
2533         } 
2534     } 
2535 } 
2536  
2537 /** 
2538  * @brief Learn whether a list instance has all the keys. 
2539  * 
2540  * @param[in] list List instance to check. 
2541  * @return non-zero if all the keys were found, 
2542  * @return 0 otherwise. 
2543  */ 
2544 static int 
2545 lyd_insert_has_keys(const struct lyd_node *list) 
2546 { 
2547     const struct lyd_node *key; 
2548     const struct lysc_node *skey = NULL; 
2549  
2550     assert(list->schema->nodetype == LYS_LIST); 
2551     key = lyd_child(list); 
2552     while ((skey = lys_getnext(skey, list->schema, NULL, 0)) && (skey->flags & LYS_KEY)) { 
2553         if (!key || (key->schema != skey)) { 
2554             /* key missing */ 
2555             return 0; 
2556         } 
2557  
2558         key = key->next; 
2559     } 
2560  
2561     /* all keys found */ 
2562     return 1; 
2563 } 
2564  
2565 void 
2566 lyd_insert_node(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_node **first_sibling_p, struct lyd_node *node, ly_bool last) 
2567 { 
2568     struct lyd_node *anchor, *first_sibling; 
2569  
2570     /* inserting list without its keys is not supported */ 
2571     assert((parent || first_sibling_p) && node && (node->hash || !node->schema)); 
2572     assert(!parent || !parent->schema || 
2573             (parent->schema->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LIST | LYS_RPC | LYS_ACTION | LYS_NOTIF))); 
2574  
2575     if (!parent && first_sibling_p && (*first_sibling_p) && (*first_sibling_p)->parent) { 
2576         parent = lyd_parent(*first_sibling_p); 
2577     } 
2578  
2579     /* get first sibling */ 
2580     first_sibling = parent ? lyd_child(parent) : *first_sibling_p; 
2581  
2582     if (last) { 
2583         /* no next anchor */ 
2584         anchor = NULL; 
2585     } else { 
2586         /* find the anchor, our next node, so we can insert before it */ 
2587         anchor = lyd_insert_get_next_anchor(first_sibling, node); 
2588     } 
2589  
2590     if (anchor) { 
2591         /* insert before the anchor */ 
2592         lyd_insert_before_node(anchor, node); 
2593         if (!parent && (*first_sibling_p == anchor)) { 
2594             /* move first sibling */ 
2595             *first_sibling_p = node; 
2596         } 
2597     } else if (first_sibling) { 
2598         /* insert as the last node */ 
2599         lyd_insert_after_node(first_sibling->prev, node); 
2600     } else if (parent) { 
2601         /* insert as the only child */ 
2602         lyd_insert_only_child(parent, node); 
2603     } else { 
2604         /* insert as the only sibling */ 
2605         *first_sibling_p = node; 
2606     } 
2607  
2608     /* insert into parent HT */ 
2609     lyd_insert_hash(node); 
2610  
2611     /* finish hashes for our parent, if needed and possible */ 
2612     if (node->schema && (node->schema->flags & LYS_KEY) && parent && lyd_insert_has_keys(parent)) { 
2613         lyd_hash(parent); 
2614  
2615         /* now we can insert even the list into its parent HT */ 
2616         lyd_insert_hash(parent); 
2617     } 
2618 } 
2619  
2620 /** 
2621  * @brief Check schema place of a node to be inserted. 
2622  * 
2623  * @param[in] parent Schema node of the parent data node. 
2624  * @param[in] sibling Schema node of a sibling data node. 
2625  * @param[in] schema Schema node if the data node to be inserted. 
2626  * @return LY_SUCCESS on success. 
2627  * @return LY_EINVAL if the place is invalid. 
2628  */ 
2629 static LY_ERR 
2630 lyd_insert_check_schema(const struct lysc_node *parent, const struct lysc_node *sibling, const struct lysc_node *schema) 
2631 { 
2632     const struct lysc_node *par2; 
2633  
2634     assert(!parent || !(parent->nodetype & (LYS_CASE | LYS_CHOICE))); 
2635     assert(!sibling || !(sibling->nodetype & (LYS_CASE | LYS_CHOICE))); 
2636     assert(!schema || !(schema->nodetype & (LYS_CASE | LYS_CHOICE))); 
2637  
2638     if (!schema || (!parent && !sibling)) { 
2639         /* opaque nodes can be inserted wherever */ 
2640         return LY_SUCCESS; 
2641     } 
2642  
2643     if (!parent) { 
2644         parent = lysc_data_parent(sibling); 
2645     } 
2646  
2647     /* find schema parent */ 
2648     par2 = lysc_data_parent(schema); 
2649  
2650     if (parent) { 
2651         /* inner node */ 
2652         if (par2 != parent) { 
2653             LOGERR(schema->module->ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Cannot insert, parent of \"%s\" is not \"%s\".", schema->name, 
2654                     parent->name); 
2655             return LY_EINVAL; 
2656         } 
2657     } else { 
2658         /* top-level node */ 
2659         if (par2) { 
2660             LOGERR(schema->module->ctx, LY_EINVAL, "Cannot insert, node \"%s\" is not top-level.", schema->name); 
2661             return LY_EINVAL; 
2662         } 
2663     } 
2664  
2665     return LY_SUCCESS; 
2666 } 
2667  
2668 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2669 lyd_insert_child(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_node *node) 
2670 { 
2671     struct lyd_node *iter; 
2672  
2673     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, parent, node, !parent->schema || (parent->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_INNER), LY_EINVAL); 
2674     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(LYD_CTX(parent), LYD_CTX(node), LY_EINVAL); 
2675  
2676     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_insert_check_schema(parent->schema, NULL, node->schema)); 
2677  
2678     if (node->schema && (node->schema->flags & LYS_KEY)) { 
2679         LOGERR(LYD_CTX(parent), LY_EINVAL, "Cannot insert key \"%s\".", node->schema->name); 
2680         return LY_EINVAL; 
2681     } 
2682  
2683     if (node->parent || node->prev->next) { 
2684         lyd_unlink_tree(node); 
2685     } 
2686  
2687     while (node) { 
2688         iter = node->next; 
2689         lyd_unlink_tree(node); 
2690         lyd_insert_node(parent, NULL, node, 0); 
2691         node = iter; 
2692     } 
2693     return LY_SUCCESS; 
2694 } 
2695  
2696 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2697 lyd_insert_ext(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_node *first) 
2698 { 
2699     struct lyd_node *iter; 
2700  
2701     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, parent, first, !first->parent, !first->prev->next, 
2702             !parent->schema || (parent->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_INNER), LY_EINVAL); 
2703  
2704     if (first->schema && (first->schema->flags & LYS_KEY)) { 
2705         LOGERR(LYD_CTX(parent), LY_EINVAL, "Cannot insert key \"%s\".", first->schema->name); 
2706         return LY_EINVAL; 
2707     } 
2708  
2709     while (first) { 
2710         iter = first->next; 
2711         lyd_unlink_tree(first); 
2712         lyd_insert_node(parent, NULL, first, 1); 
2713         first = iter; 
2714     } 
2715     return LY_SUCCESS; 
2716 } 
2717  
2718 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2719 lyd_insert_sibling(struct lyd_node *sibling, struct lyd_node *node, struct lyd_node **first) 
2720 { 
2721     struct lyd_node *iter; 
2722  
2723     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, node, LY_EINVAL); 
2724     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(sibling ? LYD_CTX(sibling) : NULL, LYD_CTX(node), LY_EINVAL); 
2725  
2726     if (sibling) { 
2727         LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_insert_check_schema(NULL, sibling->schema, node->schema)); 
2728     } 
2729  
2730     if (sibling == node) { 
2731         /* we need to keep the connection to siblings so we can insert into them */ 
2732         sibling = sibling->prev; 
2733     } 
2734  
2735     if (node->parent || node->prev->next) { 
2736         lyd_unlink_tree(node); 
2737     } 
2738  
2739     while (node) { 
2740         if (lysc_is_key(node->schema)) { 
2741             LOGERR(LYD_CTX(node), LY_EINVAL, "Cannot insert key \"%s\".", node->schema->name); 
2742             return LY_EINVAL; 
2743         } 
2744  
2745         iter = node->next; 
2746         lyd_unlink_tree(node); 
2747         lyd_insert_node(NULL, &sibling, node, 0); 
2748         node = iter; 
2749     } 
2750  
2751     if (first) { 
2752         /* find the first sibling */ 
2753         *first = sibling; 
2754         while ((*first)->prev->next) { 
2755             *first = (*first)->prev; 
2756         } 
2757     } 
2758  
2759     return LY_SUCCESS; 
2760 } 
2761  
2762 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2763 lyd_insert_before(struct lyd_node *sibling, struct lyd_node *node) 
2764 { 
2765     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, sibling, node, LY_EINVAL); 
2766     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(LYD_CTX(sibling), LYD_CTX(node), LY_EINVAL); 
2767  
2768     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_insert_check_schema(NULL, sibling->schema, node->schema)); 
2769  
2770     if (!(node->schema->nodetype & (LYS_LIST | LYS_LEAFLIST)) || !(node->schema->flags & LYS_ORDBY_USER)) { 
2771         LOGERR(LYD_CTX(sibling), LY_EINVAL, "Can be used only for user-ordered nodes."); 
2772         return LY_EINVAL; 
2773     } 
2774  
2775     lyd_unlink_tree(node); 
2776     lyd_insert_before_node(sibling, node); 
2777     lyd_insert_hash(node); 
2778  
2779     return LY_SUCCESS; 
2780 } 
2781  
2782 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
2783 lyd_insert_after(struct lyd_node *sibling, struct lyd_node *node) 
2784 { 
2785     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, sibling, node, LY_EINVAL); 
2786     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(LYD_CTX(sibling), LYD_CTX(node), LY_EINVAL); 
2787  
2788     LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_insert_check_schema(NULL, sibling->schema, node->schema)); 
2789  
2790     if (!(node->schema->nodetype & (LYS_LIST | LYS_LEAFLIST)) || !(node->schema->flags & LYS_ORDBY_USER)) { 
2791         LOGERR(LYD_CTX(sibling), LY_EINVAL, "Can be used only for user-ordered nodes."); 
2792         return LY_EINVAL; 
2793     } 
2794  
2795     lyd_unlink_tree(node); 
2796     lyd_insert_after_node(sibling, node); 
2797     lyd_insert_hash(node); 
2798  
2799     return LY_SUCCESS; 
2800 } 
2801  
2802 LIBYANG_API_DEF void 
2803 lyd_unlink_siblings(struct lyd_node *node) 
2804 { 
2805     struct lyd_node *next, *elem, *first = NULL; 
2806  
2807     LY_LIST_FOR_SAFE(node, next, elem) { 
2808         lyd_unlink_tree(elem); 
2809         lyd_insert_node(NULL, &first, elem, 1); 
2810     } 
2811 } 
2812  
2813 LIBYANG_API_DEF void 
2814 lyd_unlink_tree(struct lyd_node *node) 
2815 { 
2816     struct lyd_node *iter; 
2817  
2818     if (!node) { 
2819         return; 
2820     } 
2821  
2822     /* update hashes while still linked into the tree */ 
2823     lyd_unlink_hash(node); 
2824  
2825     /* unlink from siblings */ 
2826     if (node->prev->next) { 
2827         node->prev->next = node->next; 
2828     } 
2829     if (node->next) { 
2830         node->next->prev = node->prev; 
2831     } else { 
2832         /* unlinking the last node */ 
2833         if (node->parent) { 
2834             iter = node->parent->child; 
2835         } else { 
2836             iter = node->prev; 
2837             while (iter->prev != node) { 
2838                 iter = iter->prev; 
2839             } 
2840         } 
2841         /* update the "last" pointer from the first node */ 
2842         iter->prev = node->prev; 
2843     } 
2844  
2845     /* unlink from parent */ 
2846     if (node->parent) { 
2847         if (node->parent->child == node) { 
2848             /* the node is the first child */ 
2849             node->parent->child = node->next; 
2850         } 
2851  
2852         /* check for NP container whether its last non-default node is not being unlinked */ 
2853         if (node->parent->schema && (node->parent->schema->nodetype == LYS_CONTAINER) && 
2854                 !(node->parent->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) && !(node->parent->schema->flags & LYS_PRESENCE)) { 
2855             LY_LIST_FOR(node->parent->child, iter) { 
2856                 if ((iter != node) && !(iter->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
2857                     break; 
2858                 } 
2859             } 
2860             if (!iter) { 
2861                 node->parent->flags |= LYD_DEFAULT; 
2862             } 
2863         } 
2864  
2865         node->parent = NULL; 
2866     } 
2867  
2868     node->next = NULL; 
2869     node->prev = node; 
2870 } 
2871  
2872 void 
2873 lyd_insert_meta(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_meta *meta, ly_bool clear_dflt) 
2874 { 
2875     struct lyd_meta *last, *iter; 
2876  
2877     assert(parent); 
2878  
2879     if (!meta) { 
2880         return; 
2881     } 
2882  
2883     for (iter = meta; iter; iter = iter->next) { 
2884         iter->parent = parent; 
2885     } 
2886  
2887     /* insert as the last attribute */ 
2888     if (parent->meta) { 
2889         for (last = parent->meta; last->next; last = last->next) {} 
2890         last->next = meta; 
2891     } else { 
2892         parent->meta = meta; 
2893     } 
2894  
2895     /* remove default flags from NP containers */ 
2896     while (clear_dflt && parent && (parent->schema->nodetype == LYS_CONTAINER) && (parent->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
2897         parent->flags &= ~LYD_DEFAULT; 
2898         parent = lyd_parent(parent); 
2899     } 
2900 } 
2901  
2902 LY_ERR 
2903 lyd_create_meta(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_meta **meta, const struct lys_module *mod, const char *name, 
2904         size_t name_len, const char *value, size_t value_len, ly_bool *dynamic, LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, 
2905         void *prefix_data, uint32_t hints, const struct lysc_node *ctx_node, ly_bool clear_dflt, ly_bool *incomplete) 
2906 { 
2907     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
2908     struct lysc_ext_instance *ant = NULL; 
2909     const struct lysc_type **ant_type; 
2910     struct lyd_meta *mt, *last; 
2911     LY_ARRAY_COUNT_TYPE u; 
2912  
2913     assert((parent || meta) && mod); 
2914  
2915     LOG_LOCSET(ctx_node, parent, NULL, NULL); 
2916  
2917     LY_ARRAY_FOR(mod->compiled->exts, u) { 
2918         if ((mod->compiled->exts[u].def->plugin == lyplg_find(LYPLG_EXTENSION, LYEXT_PLUGIN_INTERNAL_ANNOTATION)) && 
2919                 !ly_strncmp(mod->compiled->exts[u].argument, name, name_len)) { 
2920             /* we have the annotation definition */ 
2921             ant = &mod->compiled->exts[u]; 
2922             break; 
2923         } 
2924     } 
2925     if (!ant) { 
2926         /* attribute is not defined as a metadata annotation (RFC 7952) */ 
2927         LOGVAL(mod->ctx, LYVE_REFERENCE, "Annotation definition for attribute \"%s:%.*s\" not found.", 
2928                 mod->name, (int)name_len, name); 
2929         ret = LY_EINVAL; 
2930         goto cleanup; 
2931     } 
2932  
2933     mt = calloc(1, sizeof *mt); 
2934     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!mt, LOGMEM(mod->ctx); ret = LY_EMEM, cleanup); 
2935     mt->parent = parent; 
2936     mt->annotation = ant; 
2937     ant_type = ant->substmts[ANNOTATION_SUBSTMT_TYPE].storage; 
2938     ret = lyd_value_store(mod->ctx, &mt->value, *ant_type, value, value_len, dynamic, format, prefix_data, hints, 
2939             ctx_node, incomplete); 
2940     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(ret, free(mt), cleanup); 
2941     ret = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, name, name_len, &mt->name); 
2942     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(ret, free(mt), cleanup); 
2943  
2944     /* insert as the last attribute */ 
2945     if (parent) { 
2946         lyd_insert_meta(parent, mt, clear_dflt); 
2947     } else if (*meta) { 
2948         for (last = *meta; last->next; last = last->next) {} 
2949         last->next = mt; 
2950     } 
2951  
2952     if (meta) { 
2953         *meta = mt; 
2954     } 
2955  
2956 cleanup: 
2957     LOG_LOCBACK(ctx_node ? 1 : 0, parent ? 1 : 0, 0, 0); 
2958     return ret; 
2959 } 
2960  
2961 void 
2962 lyd_insert_attr(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_attr *attr) 
2963 { 
2964     struct lyd_attr *last, *iter; 
2965     struct lyd_node_opaq *opaq; 
2966  
2967     assert(parent && !parent->schema); 
2968  
2969     if (!attr) { 
2970         return; 
2971     } 
2972  
2973     opaq = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)parent; 
2974     for (iter = attr; iter; iter = iter->next) { 
2975         iter->parent = opaq; 
2976     } 
2977  
2978     /* insert as the last attribute */ 
2979     if (opaq->attr) { 
2980         for (last = opaq->attr; last->next; last = last->next) {} 
2981         last->next = attr; 
2982     } else { 
2983         opaq->attr = attr; 
2984     } 
2985 } 
2986  
2987 LY_ERR 
2988 lyd_create_attr(struct lyd_node *parent, struct lyd_attr **attr, const struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *name, size_t name_len, 
2989         const char *prefix, size_t prefix_len, const char *module_key, size_t module_key_len, const char *value, 
2990         size_t value_len, ly_bool *dynamic, LY_VALUE_FORMAT format, void *val_prefix_data, uint32_t hints) 
2991 { 
2992     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
2993     struct lyd_attr *at, *last; 
2994  
2995     assert(ctx && (parent || attr) && (!parent || !parent->schema)); 
2996     assert(name && name_len && format); 
2997  
2998     if (!value_len && (!dynamic || !*dynamic)) { 
2999         value = ""; 
3000     } 
3001  
3002     at = calloc(1, sizeof *at); 
3003     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!at, LOGMEM(ctx); ly_free_prefix_data(format, val_prefix_data), LY_EMEM); 
3004  
3005     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, name, name_len, &at->name.name), finish); 
3006     if (prefix_len) { 
3007         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, prefix, prefix_len, &at->name.prefix), finish); 
3008     } 
3009     if (module_key_len) { 
3010         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, module_key, module_key_len, &at->name.module_ns), finish); 
3011     } 
3012  
3013     if (dynamic && *dynamic) { 
3014         ret = lydict_insert_zc(ctx, (char *)value, &at->value); 
3015         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret, finish); 
3016         *dynamic = 0; 
3017     } else { 
3018         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(ctx, value, value_len, &at->value), finish); 
3019     } 
3020     at->format = format; 
3021     at->val_prefix_data = val_prefix_data; 
3022     at->hints = hints; 
3023  
3024     /* insert as the last attribute */ 
3025     if (parent) { 
3026         lyd_insert_attr(parent, at); 
3027     } else if (*attr) { 
3028         for (last = *attr; last->next; last = last->next) {} 
3029         last->next = at; 
3030     } 
3031  
3032 finish: 
3033     if (ret) { 
3034         lyd_free_attr_single(ctx, at); 
3035     } else if (attr) { 
3036         *attr = at; 
3037     } 
3038     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3039 } 
3040  
3041 LIBYANG_API_DEF const struct lyd_node_term * 
3042 lyd_target(const struct ly_path *path, const struct lyd_node *tree) 
3043 { 
3044     struct lyd_node *target; 
3045  
3046     if (ly_path_eval(path, tree, &target)) { 
3047         return NULL; 
3048     } 
3049  
3050     return (struct lyd_node_term *)target; 
3051 } 
3052  
3053 /** 
3054  * @brief Check the equality of the two schemas from different contexts. 
3055  * 
3056  * @param schema1 of first node. 
3057  * @param schema2 of second node. 
3058  * @return 1 if the schemas are equal otherwise 0. 
3059  */ 
3060 static ly_bool 
3061 lyd_compare_schema_equal(const struct lysc_node *schema1, const struct lysc_node *schema2) 
3062 { 
3063     if (!schema1 && !schema2) { 
3064         return 1; 
3065     } else if (!schema1 || !schema2) { 
3066         return 0; 
3067     } 
3068  
3069     assert(schema1->module->ctx != schema2->module->ctx); 
3070  
3071     if (schema1->nodetype != schema2->nodetype) { 
3072         return 0; 
3073     } 
3074  
3075     if (strcmp(schema1->name, schema2->name)) { 
3076         return 0; 
3077     } 
3078  
3079     if (strcmp(schema1->module->name, schema2->module->name)) { 
3080         return 0; 
3081     } 
3082  
3083     if (schema1->module->revision || schema2->module->revision) { 
3084         if (!schema1->module->revision || !schema2->module->revision) { 
3085             return 0; 
3086         } 
3087         if (strcmp(schema1->module->revision, schema2->module->revision)) { 
3088             return 0; 
3089         } 
3090     } 
3091  
3092     return 1; 
3093 } 
3094  
3095 /** 
3096  * @brief Check the equality of the schemas for all parent nodes. 
3097  * 
3098  * Both nodes must be from different contexts. 
3099  * 
3100  * @param node1 Data of first node. 
3101  * @param node2 Data of second node. 
3102  * @return 1 if the all related parental schemas are equal otherwise 0. 
3103  */ 
3104 static ly_bool 
3105 lyd_compare_schema_parents_equal(const struct lyd_node *node1, const struct lyd_node *node2) 
3106 { 
3107     const struct lysc_node *parent1, *parent2; 
3108  
3109     assert(node1 && node2); 
3110  
3111     for (parent1 = node1->schema->parent, parent2 = node2->schema->parent; 
3112             parent1 && parent2; 
3113             parent1 = parent1->parent, parent2 = parent2->parent) { 
3114         if (!lyd_compare_schema_equal(parent1, parent2)) { 
3115             return 0; 
3116         } 
3117     } 
3118  
3119     if (parent1 || parent2) { 
3120         return 0; 
3121     } 
3122  
3123     return 1; 
3124 } 
3125  
3126 /** 
3127  * @brief Internal implementation of @ref lyd_compare_single. 
3128  * @copydoc lyd_compare_single 
3129  * @param[in] parental_schemas_checked Flag used for optimization. 
3130  * When this function is called for the first time, the flag must be set to 0. 
3131  * The @ref lyd_compare_schema_parents_equal should be called only once during 
3132  * recursive calls, and this is accomplished by setting to 1 in the lyd_compare_single_ body. 
3133  */ 
3134 static LY_ERR 
3135 lyd_compare_single_(const struct lyd_node *node1, const struct lyd_node *node2, 
3136         uint32_t options, ly_bool parental_schemas_checked) 
3137 { 
3138     const struct lyd_node *iter1, *iter2; 
3139     struct lyd_node_term *term1, *term2; 
3140     struct lyd_node_any *any1, *any2; 
3141     struct lyd_node_opaq *opaq1, *opaq2; 
3142     size_t len1, len2; 
3143  
3144     if (!node1 || !node2) { 
3145         if (node1 == node2) { 
3146             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3147         } else { 
3148             return LY_ENOT; 
3149         } 
3150     } 
3151  
3152     if (LYD_CTX(node1) == LYD_CTX(node2)) { 
3153         /* same contexts */ 
3154         if (node1->schema != node2->schema) { 
3155             return LY_ENOT; 
3156         } 
3157     } else { 
3158         /* different contexts */ 
3159         if (!lyd_compare_schema_equal(node1->schema, node2->schema)) { 
3160             return LY_ENOT; 
3161         } 
3162         if (!parental_schemas_checked) { 
3163             if (!lyd_compare_schema_parents_equal(node1, node2)) { 
3164                 return LY_ENOT; 
3165             } 
3166             parental_schemas_checked = 1; 
3167         } 
3168     } 
3169  
3170     if (node1->hash != node2->hash) { 
3171         return LY_ENOT; 
3172     } 
3173     /* equal hashes do not mean equal nodes, they can be just in collision so the nodes must be checked explicitly */ 
3174  
3175     if (!node1->schema) { 
3176         opaq1 = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)node1; 
3177         opaq2 = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)node2; 
3178         if ((strcmp(opaq1->name.name, opaq2->name.name)) || (opaq1->format != opaq2->format) || 
3179                 (strcmp(opaq1->name.module_ns, opaq2->name.module_ns))) { 
3180             return LY_ENOT; 
3181         } 
3182         switch (opaq1->format) { 
3183         case LY_VALUE_XML: 
3184             if (lyxml_value_compare(LYD_CTX(node1), opaq1->value, opaq1->val_prefix_data, LYD_CTX(node2), opaq2->value, 
3185                     opaq2->val_prefix_data)) { 
3186                 return LY_ENOT; 
3187             } 
3188             break; 
3189         case LY_VALUE_JSON: 
3190             /* prefixes in JSON are unique, so it is not necessary to canonize the values */ 
3191             if (strcmp(opaq1->value, opaq2->value)) { 
3192                 return LY_ENOT; 
3193             } 
3194             break; 
3195         default: 
3196             /* not allowed */ 
3197             LOGINT(LYD_CTX(node1)); 
3198             return LY_EINT; 
3199         } 
3200         if (options & LYD_COMPARE_FULL_RECURSION) { 
3201             iter1 = opaq1->child; 
3202             iter2 = opaq2->child; 
3203             goto all_children_compare; 
3204         } 
3205         return LY_SUCCESS; 
3206     } else { 
3207         switch (node1->schema->nodetype) { 
3208         case LYS_LEAF: 
3209         case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
3210             if (options & LYD_COMPARE_DEFAULTS) { 
3211                 if ((node1->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) != (node2->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
3212                     return LY_ENOT; 
3213                 } 
3214             } 
3215  
3216             term1 = (struct lyd_node_term *)node1; 
3217             term2 = (struct lyd_node_term *)node2; 
3218  
3219             /* same contexts */ 
3220             if (LYD_CTX(node1) == LYD_CTX(node2)) { 
3221                 return term1->value.realtype->plugin->compare(&term1->value, &term2->value); 
3222             } 
3223  
3224             /* different contexts */ 
3225             if (strcmp(lyd_get_value(node1), lyd_get_value(node2))) { 
3226                 return LY_ENOT; 
3227             } 
3228             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3229         case LYS_CONTAINER: 
3230             if (options & LYD_COMPARE_DEFAULTS) { 
3231                 if ((node1->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) != (node2->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
3232                     return LY_ENOT; 
3233                 } 
3234             } 
3235             if (options & LYD_COMPARE_FULL_RECURSION) { 
3236                 iter1 = lyd_child(node1); 
3237                 iter2 = lyd_child(node2); 
3238                 goto all_children_compare; 
3239             } 
3240             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3241         case LYS_RPC: 
3242         case LYS_ACTION: 
3243             if (options & LYD_COMPARE_FULL_RECURSION) { 
3244                 /* TODO action/RPC 
3245                 goto all_children_compare; 
3246                 */ 
3247             } 
3248             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3249         case LYS_NOTIF: 
3250             if (options & LYD_COMPARE_FULL_RECURSION) { 
3251                 /* TODO Notification 
3252                 goto all_children_compare; 
3253                 */ 
3254             } 
3255             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3256         case LYS_LIST: 
3257             iter1 = lyd_child(node1); 
3258             iter2 = lyd_child(node2); 
3259  
3260             if (!(node1->schema->flags & LYS_KEYLESS) && !(options & LYD_COMPARE_FULL_RECURSION)) { 
3261                 /* lists with keys, their equivalence is based on their keys */ 
3262                 for (const struct lysc_node *key = lysc_node_child(node1->schema); 
3263                         key && (key->flags & LYS_KEY); 
3264                         key = key->next) { 
3265                     if (lyd_compare_single_(iter1, iter2, options, parental_schemas_checked)) { 
3266                         return LY_ENOT; 
3267                     } 
3268                     iter1 = iter1->next; 
3269                     iter2 = iter2->next; 
3270                 } 
3271             } else { 
3272                 /* lists without keys, their equivalence is based on equivalence of all the children (both direct and indirect) */ 
3273  
3274 all_children_compare: 
3275                 if (!iter1 && !iter2) { 
3276                     /* no children, nothing to compare */ 
3277                     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3278                 } 
3279  
3280                 for ( ; iter1 && iter2; iter1 = iter1->next, iter2 = iter2->next) { 
3281                     if (lyd_compare_single_(iter1, iter2, options | LYD_COMPARE_FULL_RECURSION, parental_schemas_checked)) { 
3282                         return LY_ENOT; 
3283                     } 
3284                 } 
3285                 if (iter1 || iter2) { 
3286                     return LY_ENOT; 
3287                 } 
3288             } 
3289             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3290         case LYS_ANYXML: 
3291         case LYS_ANYDATA: 
3292             any1 = (struct lyd_node_any *)node1; 
3293             any2 = (struct lyd_node_any *)node2; 
3294  
3295             if (any1->value_type != any2->value_type) { 
3296                 return LY_ENOT; 
3297             } 
3298             switch (any1->value_type) { 
3299             case LYD_ANYDATA_DATATREE: 
3300                 iter1 = any1->value.tree; 
3301                 iter2 = any2->value.tree; 
3302                 goto all_children_compare; 
3303             case LYD_ANYDATA_STRING: 
3304             case LYD_ANYDATA_XML: 
3305             case LYD_ANYDATA_JSON: 
3306                 len1 = strlen(any1->value.str); 
3307                 len2 = strlen(any2->value.str); 
3308                 if ((len1 != len2) || strcmp(any1->value.str, any2->value.str)) { 
3309                     return LY_ENOT; 
3310                 } 
3311                 return LY_SUCCESS; 
3312             case LYD_ANYDATA_LYB: 
3313                 len1 = lyd_lyb_data_length(any1->value.mem); 
3314                 len2 = lyd_lyb_data_length(any2->value.mem); 
3315                 if ((len1 != len2) || memcmp(any1->value.mem, any2->value.mem, len1)) { 
3316                     return LY_ENOT; 
3317                 } 
3318                 return LY_SUCCESS; 
3319             } 
3320         } 
3321     } 
3322  
3323     LOGINT(LYD_CTX(node1)); 
3324     return LY_EINT; 
3325 } 
3326  
3327 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3328 lyd_compare_single(const struct lyd_node *node1, const struct lyd_node *node2, uint32_t options) 
3329 { 
3330     return lyd_compare_single_(node1, node2, options, 0); 
3331 } 
3332  
3333 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3334 lyd_compare_siblings(const struct lyd_node *node1, const struct lyd_node *node2, uint32_t options) 
3335 { 
3336     for ( ; node1 && node2; node1 = node1->next, node2 = node2->next) { 
3337         LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_compare_single(node1, node2, options)); 
3338     } 
3339  
3340     if (node1 == node2) { 
3341         return LY_SUCCESS; 
3342     } 
3343     return LY_ENOT; 
3344 } 
3345  
3346 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3347 lyd_compare_meta(const struct lyd_meta *meta1, const struct lyd_meta *meta2) 
3348 { 
3349     if (!meta1 || !meta2) { 
3350         if (meta1 == meta2) { 
3351             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3352         } else { 
3353             return LY_ENOT; 
3354         } 
3355     } 
3356  
3357     if ((meta1->annotation->module->ctx != meta2->annotation->module->ctx) || (meta1->annotation != meta2->annotation)) { 
3358         return LY_ENOT; 
3359     } 
3360  
3361     return meta1->value.realtype->plugin->compare(&meta1->value, &meta2->value); 
3362 } 
3363  
3364 /** 
3365  * @brief Create a copy of the attribute. 
3366  * 
3367  * @param[in] attr Attribute to copy. 
3368  * @param[in] node Opaque where to append the new attribute. 
3369  * @param[out] dup Optional created attribute copy. 
3370  * @return LY_ERR value. 
3371  */ 
3372 static LY_ERR 
3373 lyd_dup_attr_single(const struct lyd_attr *attr, struct lyd_node *node, struct lyd_attr **dup) 
3374 { 
3375     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
3376     struct lyd_attr *a, *last; 
3377     struct lyd_node_opaq *opaq = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)node; 
3378  
3379     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, attr, node, !node->schema, LY_EINVAL); 
3380  
3381     /* create a copy */ 
3382     a = calloc(1, sizeof *attr); 
3383     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!a, LOGMEM(LYD_CTX(node)), LY_EMEM); 
3384  
3385     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(LYD_CTX(node), attr->name.name, 0, &a->name.name), finish); 
3386     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(LYD_CTX(node), attr->name.prefix, 0, &a->name.prefix), finish); 
3387     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(LYD_CTX(node), attr->name.module_ns, 0, &a->name.module_ns), finish); 
3388     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(LYD_CTX(node), attr->value, 0, &a->value), finish); 
3389     a->hints = attr->hints; 
3390     a->format = attr->format; 
3391     if (attr->val_prefix_data) { 
3392         ret = ly_dup_prefix_data(LYD_CTX(node), attr->format, attr->val_prefix_data, &a->val_prefix_data); 
3393         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret, finish); 
3394     } 
3395  
3396     /* insert as the last attribute */ 
3397     a->parent = opaq; 
3398     if (opaq->attr) { 
3399         for (last = opaq->attr; last->next; last = last->next) {} 
3400         last->next = a; 
3401     } else { 
3402         opaq->attr = a; 
3403     } 
3404  
3405 finish: 
3406     if (ret) { 
3407         lyd_free_attr_single(LYD_CTX(node), a); 
3408     } else if (dup) { 
3409         *dup = a; 
3410     } 
3411     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3412 } 
3413  
3414 /** 
3415  * @brief Find @p schema equivalent in @p trg_ctx. 
3416  * 
3417  * @param[in] schema Schema node to find. 
3418  * @param[in] trg_ctx Target context to search in. 
3419  * @param[in] parent Data parent of @p schema, if any. 
3420  * @param[out] trg_schema Found schema from @p trg_ctx to use. 
3421  * @return LY_RRR value. 
3422  */ 
3423 static LY_ERR 
3424 lyd_dup_find_schema(const struct lysc_node *schema, const struct ly_ctx *trg_ctx, struct lyd_node *parent, 
3425         const struct lysc_node **trg_schema) 
3426 { 
3427     const struct lysc_node *node = NULL, *sparent = NULL; 
3428     const struct lys_module *mod = NULL; 
3429     char *path; 
3430  
3431     if (!schema) { 
3432         /* opaque node */ 
3433         *trg_schema = NULL; 
3434         return LY_SUCCESS; 
3435     } 
3436  
3437     if (parent && parent->schema) { 
3438         /* start from schema parent */ 
3439         sparent = parent->schema; 
3440     } else { 
3441         /* start from module */ 
3442         mod = ly_ctx_get_module_implemented(trg_ctx, schema->module->name); 
3443         if (!mod) { 
3444             LOGERR(trg_ctx, LY_ENOTFOUND, "Module \"%s\" not present/implemented in the target context.", 
3445                     schema->module->name); 
3446             return LY_ENOTFOUND; 
3447         } 
3448     } 
3449  
3450     /* find the schema node */ 
3451     while ((node = lys_getnext(node, sparent, mod ? mod->compiled : NULL, 0))) { 
3452         if (!strcmp(node->module->name, schema->module->name) && !strcmp(node->name, schema->name)) { 
3453             *trg_schema = node; 
3454             return LY_SUCCESS; 
3455         } 
3456     } 
3457  
3458     /* schema node not found */ 
3459     path = lysc_path(schema, LYSC_PATH_LOG, NULL, 0); 
3460     LOGERR(trg_ctx, LY_ENOTFOUND, "Schema node \"%s\" not found in the target context.", path); 
3461     free(path); 
3462     return LY_ENOTFOUND; 
3463 } 
3464  
3465 /** 
3466  * @brief Duplicate a single node and connect it into @p parent (if present) or last of @p first siblings. 
3467  * 
3468  * Ignores ::LYD_DUP_WITH_PARENTS and ::LYD_DUP_WITH_SIBLINGS which are supposed to be handled by lyd_dup(). 
3469  * 
3470  * @param[in] node Node to duplicate. 
3471  * @param[in] trg_ctx Target context for duplicated nodes. 
3472  * @param[in] parent Parent to insert into, NULL for top-level sibling. 
3473  * @param[in] insert_last Whether the duplicated node can be inserted as the last child of @p parent. Set for 
3474  * recursive duplication as an optimization. 
3475  * @param[in,out] first First sibling, NULL if no top-level sibling exist yet. Can be also NULL if @p parent is set. 
3476  * @param[in] options Bitmask of options flags, see @ref dupoptions. 
3477  * @param[out] dup_p Pointer where the created duplicated node is placed (besides connecting it to @p parent / @p first). 
3478  * @return LY_ERR value. 
3479  */ 
3480 static LY_ERR 
3481 lyd_dup_r(const struct lyd_node *node, const struct ly_ctx *trg_ctx, struct lyd_node *parent, ly_bool insert_last, 
3482         struct lyd_node **first, uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **dup_p) 
3483 { 
3484     LY_ERR ret; 
3485     struct lyd_node *dup = NULL; 
3486     struct lyd_meta *meta; 
3487     struct lyd_attr *attr; 
3488     struct lyd_node_any *any; 
3489     const struct lysc_type *type; 
3490     const char *val_can; 
3491  
3492     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, node, LY_EINVAL); 
3493  
3494     if (!node->schema) { 
3495         dup = calloc(1, sizeof(struct lyd_node_opaq)); 
3496         ((struct lyd_node_opaq *)dup)->ctx = trg_ctx; 
3497     } else { 
3498         switch (node->schema->nodetype) { 
3499         case LYS_RPC: 
3500         case LYS_ACTION: 
3501         case LYS_NOTIF: 
3502         case LYS_CONTAINER: 
3503         case LYS_LIST: 
3504             dup = calloc(1, sizeof(struct lyd_node_inner)); 
3505             break; 
3506         case LYS_LEAF: 
3507         case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
3508             dup = calloc(1, sizeof(struct lyd_node_term)); 
3509             break; 
3510         case LYS_ANYDATA: 
3511         case LYS_ANYXML: 
3512             dup = calloc(1, sizeof(struct lyd_node_any)); 
3513             break; 
3514         default: 
3515             LOGINT(trg_ctx); 
3516             ret = LY_EINT; 
3517             goto error; 
3518         } 
3519     } 
3520     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!dup, LOGMEM(trg_ctx); ret = LY_EMEM, error); 
3521  
3522     if (options & LYD_DUP_WITH_FLAGS) { 
3523         dup->flags = node->flags; 
3524     } else { 
3525         dup->flags = (node->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) | LYD_NEW; 
3526     } 
3527     if (trg_ctx == LYD_CTX(node)) { 
3528         dup->schema = node->schema; 
3529     } else { 
3530         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_dup_find_schema(node->schema, trg_ctx, parent, &dup->schema), error); 
3531     } 
3532     dup->prev = dup; 
3533  
3534     /* duplicate metadata/attributes */ 
3535     if (!(options & LYD_DUP_NO_META)) { 
3536         if (!node->schema) { 
3537             LY_LIST_FOR(((struct lyd_node_opaq *)node)->attr, attr) { 
3538                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_dup_attr_single(attr, dup, NULL), error); 
3539             } 
3540         } else { 
3541             LY_LIST_FOR(node->meta, meta) { 
3542                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_dup_meta_single(meta, dup, NULL), error); 
3543             } 
3544         } 
3545     } 
3546  
3547     /* nodetype-specific work */ 
3548     if (!dup->schema) { 
3549         struct lyd_node_opaq *opaq = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)dup; 
3550         struct lyd_node_opaq *orig = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)node; 
3551         struct lyd_node *child; 
3552  
3553         if (options & LYD_DUP_RECURSIVE) { 
3554             /* duplicate all the children */ 
3555             LY_LIST_FOR(orig->child, child) { 
3556                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_dup_r(child, trg_ctx, dup, 1, NULL, options, NULL), error); 
3557             } 
3558         } 
3559         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(trg_ctx, orig->name.name, 0, &opaq->name.name), error); 
3560         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(trg_ctx, orig->name.prefix, 0, &opaq->name.prefix), error); 
3561         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(trg_ctx, orig->name.module_ns, 0, &opaq->name.module_ns), error); 
3562         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(trg_ctx, orig->value, 0, &opaq->value), error); 
3563         opaq->hints = orig->hints; 
3564         opaq->format = orig->format; 
3565         if (orig->val_prefix_data) { 
3566             ret = ly_dup_prefix_data(trg_ctx, opaq->format, orig->val_prefix_data, &opaq->val_prefix_data); 
3567             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret, error); 
3568         } 
3569     } else if (dup->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_TERM) { 
3570         struct lyd_node_term *term = (struct lyd_node_term *)dup; 
3571         struct lyd_node_term *orig = (struct lyd_node_term *)node; 
3572  
3573         term->hash = orig->hash; 
3574         if (trg_ctx == LYD_CTX(node)) { 
3575             ret = orig->value.realtype->plugin->duplicate(trg_ctx, &orig->value, &term->value); 
3576             LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(ret, LOGERR(trg_ctx, ret, "Value duplication failed."), error); 
3577         } else { 
3578             /* store canonical value in the target context */ 
3579             val_can = lyd_get_value(node); 
3580             type = ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)term->schema)->type; 
3581             ret = lyd_value_store(trg_ctx, &term->value, type, val_can, strlen(val_can), NULL, LY_VALUE_CANON, NULL, 
3582                     LYD_HINT_DATA, term->schema, NULL); 
3583             LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret, error); 
3584         } 
3585     } else if (dup->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_INNER) { 
3586         struct lyd_node_inner *orig = (struct lyd_node_inner *)node; 
3587         struct lyd_node *child; 
3588  
3589         if (options & LYD_DUP_RECURSIVE) { 
3590             /* duplicate all the children */ 
3591             LY_LIST_FOR(orig->child, child) { 
3592                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_dup_r(child, trg_ctx, dup, 1, NULL, options, NULL), error); 
3593             } 
3594         } else if ((dup->schema->nodetype == LYS_LIST) && !(dup->schema->flags & LYS_KEYLESS)) { 
3595             /* always duplicate keys of a list */ 
3596             child = orig->child; 
3597             for (const struct lysc_node *key = lysc_node_child(dup->schema); 
3598                     key && (key->flags & LYS_KEY); 
3599                     key = key->next) { 
3600                 if (!child) { 
3601                     /* possibly not keys are present in filtered tree */ 
3602                     break; 
3603                 } else if (child->schema != key) { 
3604                     /* possibly not all keys are present in filtered tree, 
3605                      * but there can be also some non-key nodes */ 
3606                     continue; 
3607                 } 
3608                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_dup_r(child, trg_ctx, dup, 1, NULL, options, NULL), error); 
3609                 child = child->next; 
3610             } 
3611         } 
3612         lyd_hash(dup); 
3613     } else if (dup->schema->nodetype & LYD_NODE_ANY) { 
3614         dup->hash = node->hash; 
3615         any = (struct lyd_node_any *)node; 
3616         LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lyd_any_copy_value(dup, &any->value, any->value_type), error); 
3617     } 
3618  
3619     /* insert */ 
3620     lyd_insert_node(parent, first, dup, insert_last); 
3621  
3622     if (dup_p) { 
3623         *dup_p = dup; 
3624     } 
3625     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3626  
3627 error: 
3628     lyd_free_tree(dup); 
3629     return ret; 
3630 } 
3631  
3632 /** 
3633  * @brief Get a parent node to connect duplicated subtree to. 
3634  * 
3635  * @param[in] node Node (subtree) to duplicate. 
3636  * @param[in] trg_ctx Target context for duplicated nodes. 
3637  * @param[in] parent Initial parent to connect to. 
3638  * @param[in] options Bitmask of options flags, see @ref dupoptions. 
3639  * @param[out] dup_parent First duplicated parent node, if any. 
3640  * @param[out] local_parent Correct parent to directly connect duplicated @p node to. 
3641  * @return LY_ERR value. 
3642  */ 
3643 static LY_ERR 
3644 lyd_dup_get_local_parent(const struct lyd_node *node, const struct ly_ctx *trg_ctx, const struct lyd_node_inner *parent, 
3645         uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **dup_parent, struct lyd_node_inner **local_parent) 
3646 { 
3647     const struct lyd_node_inner *orig_parent, *iter; 
3648     ly_bool repeat = 1; 
3649  
3650     *dup_parent = NULL; 
3651     *local_parent = NULL; 
3652  
3653     for (orig_parent = node->parent; repeat && orig_parent; orig_parent = orig_parent->parent) { 
3654         if (parent && (parent->schema == orig_parent->schema)) { 
3655             /* stop creating parents, connect what we have into the provided parent */ 
3656             iter = parent; 
3657             repeat = 0; 
3658         } else { 
3659             iter = NULL; 
3660             LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_dup_r((struct lyd_node *)orig_parent, trg_ctx, NULL, 0, (struct lyd_node **)&iter, options, 
3661                     (struct lyd_node **)&iter)); 
3662         } 
3663         if (!*local_parent) { 
3664             *local_parent = (struct lyd_node_inner *)iter; 
3665         } 
3666         if (iter->child) { 
3667             /* 1) list - add after keys 
3668              * 2) provided parent with some children */ 
3669             iter->child->prev->next = *dup_parent; 
3670             if (*dup_parent) { 
3671                 (*dup_parent)->prev = iter->child->prev; 
3672                 iter->child->prev = *dup_parent; 
3673             } 
3674         } else { 
3675             ((struct lyd_node_inner *)iter)->child = *dup_parent; 
3676         } 
3677         if (*dup_parent) { 
3678             (*dup_parent)->parent = (struct lyd_node_inner *)iter; 
3679         } 
3680         *dup_parent = (struct lyd_node *)iter; 
3681     } 
3682  
3683     if (repeat && parent) { 
3684         /* given parent and created parents chain actually do not interconnect */ 
3685         LOGERR(trg_ctx, LY_EINVAL, 
3686                 "Invalid argument parent (%s()) - does not interconnect with the created node's parents chain.", __func__); 
3687         return LY_EINVAL; 
3688     } 
3689  
3690     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3691 } 
3692  
3693 static LY_ERR 
3694 lyd_dup(const struct lyd_node *node, const struct ly_ctx *trg_ctx, struct lyd_node_inner *parent, uint32_t options, 
3695         ly_bool nosiblings, struct lyd_node **dup) 
3696 { 
3697     LY_ERR rc; 
3698     const struct lyd_node *orig;          /* original node to be duplicated */ 
3699     struct lyd_node *first = NULL;        /* the first duplicated node, this is returned */ 
3700     struct lyd_node *top = NULL;          /* the most higher created node */ 
3701     struct lyd_node_inner *local_parent = NULL; /* the direct parent node for the duplicated node(s) */ 
3702  
3703     assert(node && trg_ctx && (!parent || (LYD_CTX(parent) == trg_ctx))); 
3704  
3705     if (options & LYD_DUP_WITH_PARENTS) { 
3706         LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc = lyd_dup_get_local_parent(node, trg_ctx, parent, options & (LYD_DUP_WITH_FLAGS | LYD_DUP_NO_META), 
3707                 &top, &local_parent), error); 
3708     } else { 
3709         local_parent = parent; 
3710     } 
3711  
3712     LY_LIST_FOR(node, orig) { 
3713         if (lysc_is_key(orig->schema)) { 
3714             if (local_parent) { 
3715                 /* the key must already exist in the parent */ 
3716                 rc = lyd_find_sibling_schema(local_parent->child, orig->schema, first ? NULL : &first); 
3717                 LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(rc, LOGINT(trg_ctx), error); 
3718             } else { 
3719                 assert(!(options & LYD_DUP_WITH_PARENTS)); 
3720                 /* duplicating a single key, okay, I suppose... */ 
3721                 rc = lyd_dup_r(orig, trg_ctx, NULL, 0, &first, options, first ? NULL : &first); 
3722                 LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc, error); 
3723             } 
3724         } else { 
3725             /* if there is no local parent, it will be inserted into first */ 
3726             rc = lyd_dup_r(orig, trg_ctx, local_parent ? &local_parent->node : NULL, 0, &first, options, 
3727                     first ? NULL : &first); 
3728             LY_CHECK_GOTO(rc, error); 
3729         } 
3730         if (nosiblings) { 
3731             break; 
3732         } 
3733     } 
3734  
3735     if (dup) { 
3736         *dup = first; 
3737     } 
3738     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3739  
3740 error: 
3741     if (top) { 
3742         lyd_free_tree(top); 
3743     } else { 
3744         lyd_free_siblings(first); 
3745     } 
3746     return rc; 
3747 } 
3748  
3749 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3750 lyd_dup_single(const struct lyd_node *node, struct lyd_node_inner *parent, uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **dup) 
3751 { 
3752     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, node, LY_EINVAL); 
3753     if (node && parent && (LYD_CTX(node) != LYD_CTX(parent))) { 
3754         LOGERR(NULL, LY_EINVAL, "Different contexts used in node duplication."); 
3755         return LY_EINVAL; 
3756     } 
3757  
3758     return lyd_dup(node, LYD_CTX(node), parent, options, 1, dup); 
3759 } 
3760  
3761 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3762 lyd_dup_single_to_ctx(const struct lyd_node *node, const struct ly_ctx *trg_ctx, struct lyd_node_inner *parent, 
3763         uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **dup) 
3764 { 
3765     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(trg_ctx, node, trg_ctx, LY_EINVAL); 
3766  
3767     return lyd_dup(node, trg_ctx, parent, options, 1, dup); 
3768 } 
3769  
3770 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3771 lyd_dup_siblings(const struct lyd_node *node, struct lyd_node_inner *parent, uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **dup) 
3772 { 
3773     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, node, LY_EINVAL); 
3774     if (node && parent && (LYD_CTX(node) != LYD_CTX(parent))) { 
3775         LOGERR(NULL, LY_EINVAL, "Different contexts used in node duplication."); 
3776         return LY_EINVAL; 
3777     } 
3778  
3779     return lyd_dup(node, LYD_CTX(node), parent, options, 0, dup); 
3780 } 
3781  
3782 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3783 lyd_dup_siblings_to_ctx(const struct lyd_node *node, const struct ly_ctx *trg_ctx, struct lyd_node_inner *parent, 
3784         uint32_t options, struct lyd_node **dup) 
3785 { 
3786     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(trg_ctx, node, trg_ctx, LY_EINVAL); 
3787  
3788     return lyd_dup(node, trg_ctx, parent, options, 0, dup); 
3789 } 
3790  
3791 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
3792 lyd_dup_meta_single(const struct lyd_meta *meta, struct lyd_node *node, struct lyd_meta **dup) 
3793 { 
3794     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
3795     struct lyd_meta *mt, *last; 
3796  
3797     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, meta, node, LY_EINVAL); 
3798  
3799     /* create a copy */ 
3800     mt = calloc(1, sizeof *mt); 
3801     LY_CHECK_ERR_RET(!mt, LOGMEM(LYD_CTX(node)), LY_EMEM); 
3802     mt->annotation = meta->annotation; 
3803     ret = meta->value.realtype->plugin->duplicate(LYD_CTX(node), &meta->value, &mt->value); 
3804     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(ret, LOGERR(LYD_CTX(node), LY_EINT, "Value duplication failed."), finish); 
3805     LY_CHECK_GOTO(ret = lydict_insert(LYD_CTX(node), meta->name, 0, &mt->name), finish); 
3806  
3807     /* insert as the last attribute */ 
3808     mt->parent = node; 
3809     if (node->meta) { 
3810         for (last = node->meta; last->next; last = last->next) {} 
3811         last->next = mt; 
3812     } else { 
3813         node->meta = mt; 
3814     } 
3815  
3816 finish: 
3817     if (ret) { 
3818         lyd_free_meta_single(mt); 
3819     } else if (dup) { 
3820         *dup = mt; 
3821     } 
3822     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3823 } 
3824  
3825 /** 
3826  * @brief Merge a source sibling into target siblings. 
3827  * 
3828  * @param[in,out] first_trg First target sibling, is updated if top-level. 
3829  * @param[in] parent_trg Target parent. 
3830  * @param[in,out] sibling_src Source sibling to merge, set to NULL if spent. 
3831  * @param[in] merge_cb Optional merge callback. 
3832  * @param[in] cb_data Arbitrary callback data. 
3833  * @param[in] options Merge options. 
3834  * @param[in,out] dup_inst Duplicate instance cache for all @p first_trg siblings. 
3835  * @return LY_ERR value. 
3836  */ 
3837 static LY_ERR 
3838 lyd_merge_sibling_r(struct lyd_node **first_trg, struct lyd_node *parent_trg, const struct lyd_node **sibling_src_p, 
3839         lyd_merge_cb merge_cb, void *cb_data, uint16_t options, struct lyd_dup_inst **dup_inst) 
3840 { 
3841     const struct lyd_node *child_src, *tmp, *sibling_src; 
3842     struct lyd_node *match_trg, *dup_src, *elem; 
3843     struct lyd_node_opaq *opaq_trg, *opaq_src; 
3844     struct lysc_type *type; 
3845     struct lyd_dup_inst *child_dup_inst = NULL; 
3846     LY_ERR ret; 
3847     ly_bool first_inst = 0; 
3848  
3849     sibling_src = *sibling_src_p; 
3850     if (!sibling_src->schema) { 
3851         /* try to find the same opaque node */ 
3852         lyd_find_sibling_opaq_next(*first_trg, LYD_NAME(sibling_src), &match_trg); 
3853     } else if (sibling_src->schema->nodetype & (LYS_LIST | LYS_LEAFLIST)) { 
3854         /* try to find the exact instance */ 
3855         lyd_find_sibling_first(*first_trg, sibling_src, &match_trg); 
3856     } else { 
3857         /* try to simply find the node, there cannot be more instances */ 
3858         lyd_find_sibling_val(*first_trg, sibling_src->schema, NULL, 0, &match_trg); 
3859     } 
3860  
3861     if (match_trg) { 
3862         /* update match as needed */ 
3863         LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_dup_inst_next(&match_trg, *first_trg, dup_inst)); 
3864     } else { 
3865         /* first instance of this node */ 
3866         first_inst = 1; 
3867     } 
3868  
3869     if (match_trg) { 
3870         /* call callback */ 
3871         if (merge_cb) { 
3872             LY_CHECK_RET(merge_cb(match_trg, sibling_src, cb_data)); 
3873         } 
3874  
3875         /* node found, make sure even value matches for all node types */ 
3876         if (!match_trg->schema) { 
3877             if (lyd_compare_single(sibling_src, match_trg, 0)) { 
3878                 /* update value */ 
3879                 opaq_trg = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)match_trg; 
3880                 opaq_src = (struct lyd_node_opaq *)sibling_src; 
3881  
3882                 lydict_remove(LYD_CTX(opaq_trg), opaq_trg->value); 
3883                 lydict_insert(LYD_CTX(opaq_trg), opaq_src->value, 0, &opaq_trg->value); 
3884                 opaq_trg->hints = opaq_src->hints; 
3885  
3886                 ly_free_prefix_data(opaq_trg->format, opaq_trg->val_prefix_data); 
3887                 opaq_trg->format = opaq_src->format; 
3888                 ly_dup_prefix_data(LYD_CTX(opaq_trg), opaq_src->format, opaq_src->val_prefix_data, 
3889                         &opaq_trg->val_prefix_data); 
3890             } 
3891         } else if ((match_trg->schema->nodetype == LYS_LEAF) && 
3892                 lyd_compare_single(sibling_src, match_trg, LYD_COMPARE_DEFAULTS)) { 
3893             /* since they are different, they cannot both be default */ 
3894             assert(!(sibling_src->flags & LYD_DEFAULT) || !(match_trg->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)); 
3895  
3896             /* update value (or only LYD_DEFAULT flag) only if flag set or the source node is not default */ 
3897             if ((options & LYD_MERGE_DEFAULTS) || !(sibling_src->flags & LYD_DEFAULT)) { 
3898                 type = ((struct lysc_node_leaf *)match_trg->schema)->type; 
3899                 type->plugin->free(LYD_CTX(match_trg), &((struct lyd_node_term *)match_trg)->value); 
3900                 LY_CHECK_RET(type->plugin->duplicate(LYD_CTX(match_trg), &((struct lyd_node_term *)sibling_src)->value, 
3901                         &((struct lyd_node_term *)match_trg)->value)); 
3902  
3903                 /* copy flags and add LYD_NEW */ 
3904                 match_trg->flags = sibling_src->flags | ((options & LYD_MERGE_WITH_FLAGS) ? 0 : LYD_NEW); 
3905             } 
3906         } else if ((match_trg->schema->nodetype & LYS_ANYDATA) && lyd_compare_single(sibling_src, match_trg, 0)) { 
3907             /* update value */ 
3908             LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_any_copy_value(match_trg, &((struct lyd_node_any *)sibling_src)->value, 
3909                     ((struct lyd_node_any *)sibling_src)->value_type)); 
3910  
3911             /* copy flags and add LYD_NEW */ 
3912             match_trg->flags = sibling_src->flags | ((options & LYD_MERGE_WITH_FLAGS) ? 0 : LYD_NEW); 
3913         } 
3914  
3915         /* check descendants, recursively */ 
3916         ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
3917         LY_LIST_FOR_SAFE(lyd_child_no_keys(sibling_src), tmp, child_src) { 
3918             ret = lyd_merge_sibling_r(lyd_node_child_p(match_trg), match_trg, &child_src, merge_cb, cb_data, options, 
3919                     &child_dup_inst); 
3920             if (ret) { 
3921                 break; 
3922             } 
3923         } 
3924         lyd_dup_inst_free(child_dup_inst); 
3925         LY_CHECK_RET(ret); 
3926     } else { 
3927         /* node not found, merge it */ 
3928         if (options & LYD_MERGE_DESTRUCT) { 
3929             dup_src = (struct lyd_node *)sibling_src; 
3930             lyd_unlink_tree(dup_src); 
3931             /* spend it */ 
3932             *sibling_src_p = NULL; 
3933         } else { 
3934             LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_dup_single(sibling_src, NULL, LYD_DUP_RECURSIVE | LYD_DUP_WITH_FLAGS, &dup_src)); 
3935         } 
3936  
3937         if (!(options & LYD_MERGE_WITH_FLAGS)) { 
3938             /* set LYD_NEW for all the new nodes, required for validation */ 
3939             LYD_TREE_DFS_BEGIN(dup_src, elem) { 
3940                 elem->flags |= LYD_NEW; 
3941                 LYD_TREE_DFS_END(dup_src, elem); 
3942             } 
3943         } 
3944  
3945         /* insert */ 
3946         lyd_insert_node(parent_trg, first_trg, dup_src, 0); 
3947  
3948         if (first_inst) { 
3949             /* remember not to find this instance next time */ 
3950             LY_CHECK_RET(lyd_dup_inst_next(&dup_src, *first_trg, dup_inst)); 
3951         } 
3952  
3953         /* call callback, no source node */ 
3954         if (merge_cb) { 
3955             LY_CHECK_RET(merge_cb(dup_src, NULL, cb_data)); 
3956         } 
3957     } 
3958  
3959     return LY_SUCCESS; 
3960 } 
3961  
3962 static LY_ERR 
3963 lyd_merge(struct lyd_node **target, const struct lyd_node *source, const struct lys_module *mod, 
3964         lyd_merge_cb merge_cb, void *cb_data, uint16_t options, ly_bool nosiblings) 
3965 { 
3966     const struct lyd_node *sibling_src, *tmp; 
3967     struct lyd_dup_inst *dup_inst = NULL; 
3968     ly_bool first; 
3969     LY_ERR ret = LY_SUCCESS; 
3970  
3971     LY_CHECK_ARG_RET(NULL, target, LY_EINVAL); 
3972     LY_CHECK_CTX_EQUAL_RET(*target ? LYD_CTX(*target) : NULL, source ? LYD_CTX(source) : NULL, mod ? mod->ctx : NULL, 
3973             LY_EINVAL); 
3974  
3975     if (!source) { 
3976         /* nothing to merge */ 
3977         return LY_SUCCESS; 
3978     } 
3979  
3980     if ((*target && lysc_data_parent((*target)->schema)) || lysc_data_parent(source->schema)) { 
3981         LOGERR(LYD_CTX(source), LY_EINVAL, "Invalid arguments - can merge only 2 top-level subtrees (%s()).", __func__); 
3982         return LY_EINVAL; 
3983     } 
3984  
3985     LY_LIST_FOR_SAFE(source, tmp, sibling_src) { 
3986         if (mod && (lyd_owner_module(sibling_src) != mod)) { 
3987             /* skip data nodes from different modules */ 
3988             continue; 
3989         } 
3990  
3991         first = (sibling_src == source) ? 1 : 0; 
3992         ret = lyd_merge_sibling_r(target, NULL, &sibling_src, merge_cb, cb_data, options, &dup_inst); 
3993         if (ret) { 
3994             break; 
3995         } 
3996         if (first && !sibling_src) { 
3997             /* source was spent (unlinked), move to the next node */ 
3998             source = tmp; 
3999         } 
4000  
4001         if (nosiblings) { 
4002             break; 
4003         } 
4004     } 
4005  
4006     if (options & LYD_MERGE_DESTRUCT) { 
4007         /* free any leftover source data that were not merged */ 
4008         lyd_free_siblings((struct lyd_node *)source); 
4009     } 
4010  
4011     lyd_dup_inst_free(dup_inst); 
4012     return ret; 
4013 } 
4014  
4015 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
4016 lyd_merge_tree(struct lyd_node **target, const struct lyd_node *source, uint16_t options) 
4017 { 
4018     return lyd_merge(target, source, NULL, NULL, NULL, options, 1); 
4019 }

4021 LIBYANG_API_DEF LY_ERR 
4022 lyd_merge_siblings(struct lyd_node **target, const struct lyd_node *source, uint16_t options) 
4023 { 
4024     return lyd_merge(target, source, NULL, NULL, NULL, options, 0); 
4025 }
